Office of the Controller Examinations
Custodian of academic integrity and excellence in assessment since inception
About Examination Office
The Office of the Controller of Examinations is the principal custodian of examination records of the University of Baltistan, Skardu. We provide quality services in the context of examinations and assessments to students, faculty, and university leadership.
Ensuring
transparency, integrity, secrecy, and quality assessment service
are the core mandates of our Office. Within the framework of the HEC and under the guidelines of the Charter of the University of Baltistan, Skardu, we develop rules, policies, and procedures related to our core functions and implement them accordingly.
Digital Excellence
Through the use of the Campus Management System (CMS), the Office provides timely support and responses to queries on a regular and immediate basis.
Professional Team
Our office has a core team of highly competent professionals who are involved in quality assessment service with great zeal and zest.
Mr. Naseem Abbas
Controller of Examinations
•
MS Computer Science
Contact Information
Official Email
controller@uobs.edu.pk
Office Location
Administrative Block, Ground Floor
Key Responsibilities
Overall supervision of examination processes
Policy development and implementation
Coordination with academic departments
Final approval of examination results
Convocation planning and execution
Core Functions
Comprehensive range of examination and assessment services
Examination Leadership
Providing leadership for overall examinations and assessment
Developing and reviewing examination policies
Implementing examination procedures and rules
Quality Assurance
Liaising with HEC on assessment policies
Ensuring quality in teaching and learning
Guaranteeing safe custody of examination records
Examination Conduct
Conducting Mid-Term, Final-Term & Thesis examinations
Compiling, publishing, and disseminating final results
Preparing transcripts and degrees for successful candidates
Academic Recognition
Developing lists of Roll of Honors
Preparation of final transcripts and Degrees
Arranging convocation ceremonies
Record Management
Principal custodian of examination records

Digital record management through CMS

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Faculty of Education, Humanities and Social Sciences
Nurturing Minds, Preserving Heritage, Shaping Society
Introduction
The Faculty of Education, Humanities, and Social Sciences (FoEHSS) at the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS) lays the foundation stone for preservation and promotion of intellectual, cultural, and social development in Gilgit-Baltistan. This Faculty i. e. FoEHSS has enthusiastically been established to advancing knowledge that fortifies education systems, encourages indigenous intellect, conserves cultural heritage, and respond enduring social and economic challenges of the countrymen and the Republic.
The FoEHSS offers transdisciplinary academic programmes that blend education, languages, culture, economics, and social inquiry, cultivating critical thinking, ethical leadership, and civic responsibility. The FoEHSS prepares educators, researchers, policy practitioners, and community leaders capable of addressing to local realities while engaging with national and global discourses. Diversity and inclusion are at our core, fostering a vibrant environment where creativity and collaboration thrive. The FoEHSS makes a concerted labour enriching the educational experience of students for their success in academic life at UoBS and professional life beyond UoBS. As we look ahead, we remain committed to excellence, innovation, and shaping a better future for all.
Rooted in the unique historical, linguistic, and cultural landscape of the Karakoram–Himalayan region, the FoEHSS places strong emphasis on indigenous knowledge systems, cultural heritage, and social cohesion while promotes modern pedagogical approaches, evidence-informed research, and policy-relevant scholarship aligned with national development priorities and global frameworks such as sustainable development and global citizenship. We believe in refining intellect, preserving heritage, and forming future with the forte of inclusivity in all its manifestations.
Our Mission
To provide quality academic programmes, foster impactful research, strengthen teacher education and leadership, integrate indigenous knowledge with global perspectives, and prepare ethical graduates committed to social responsibility and transformative community development.
Our Vision
To become a premier center of excellence in education, humanities, and social sciences that bridges traditional knowledge with contemporary societal needs.
Key Focus Areas
Educational Development
Cultural Heritage
Business & Economics
Tourism Development
Balti Language Documentation
Oral Traditions
Comparative Literature
Language Preservation
Curriculum Development
Teacher Education
Dean's Message
Professor Ashfaque Ahmad SHAH PhD
Faculty of Education, Humanities and Social Sciences
Dean Since: 2021
Welcome to the Faculty of Education, Humanities and Social Sciences (FoEHSS) which represents the intellectual, cultural, and social foundation of the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS). Situated at the crossroads of South and Central Asia, Gilgit-Baltistan holds immense potential for sustainable economic development, entrepreneurship, trade, and tourism.
In a region characterised by linguistic diversity, rich cultural heritage, complex social structures, and evolving development challenges, FoEHSS plays a vital role in fostering critical thinking, cultural understanding, social cohesion, and evidence-based policymaking.
Our programmes are designed to respond directly to regional needs while meeting national and international academic standards. We promote transdisciplinary teaching and research that address regional priorities of Gilgit-Baltistan while engaging with national and global academic discourses. We urge translating values into life skills through our courses transforming competent professionals for future highly technologized information society.

University of Baltistan Celebrates Independence Day with Enthusiasm
Date
Aug 13, 2026
Venue
To be announced
Event Overview
The University of Baltistan, Skardu, has launched a two-day programme of Independence Day celebrations featuring various academic, creative, and patriotic competitions.
In his message, Vice Chancellor Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar said Independence Day is an opportunity to renew the national commitment to Pakistan’s development, stability, and intellectual progress. He appreciated the students’ enthusiasm and participation, saying that the youth are the nation’s greatest asset.
In the first round of the Urdu Speech Competition, 18 students participated, out of whom six qualified for the final based on their outstanding performances. The finalists included Nihal Ali, Urooj Fatima, and Naila Parveen from the Department of Computer Science; Pakiza Hassan and Aliza from the Department of English; and Tahira Batool from the Department of Education.
A total of 12 contestants participated in the Painting Competition and showcased their artistic abilities. Suraiya Batool from the Department of Educational Development secured first position, Zahra Batool from Software Engineering secured second position, while Shaista Jabeen from the Department of Educational Development secured third position.
The celebrations will conclude on August 14 at 10:00 a.m. with a flag-hoisting ceremony, national songs, cake cutting, and final rounds of the competitions. Certificates and prizes will be distributed among the winners at the conclusion of the programme.

Meeting
2 min read
University Reviews Academic Progress and Fall 2026 Admissions
September 4, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for University Reviews Academic Progress and Fall 2026 Admissions
Skardu September 04, 2026: A meeting of the University of Baltistan, Skardu, was held under the chairmanship of the Worthy Vice Chancellor, Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar, who participated online. The meeting focused on reviewing the progress of the ongoing semester and assessing preparations for the upcoming Fall 2026 Semester.
The meeting provided a comprehensive review of the academic progress of the ongoing semester, with particular focus on the status of teaching and learning activities, completion of academic requirements, and matters related to the effective conduct of the semester.
The participants also reviewed the status of Fall 2026 admissions, including the progress of the admission process and necessary arrangements for the commencement of the new semester. Detailed discussion was held on the preparation and implementation of the academic timetable, with emphasis on ensuring proper coordination among academic departments and timely completion of all related arrangements.
The Worthy Vice Chancellor emphasized the importance of effective academic planning, timely completion of semester activities, quality teaching and learning, and student facilitation. He directed the concerned Deans and Heads of Departments to ensure close coordination and timely implementation of the decisions taken during the meeting.
The meeting also deliberated on the Executive MBA Weekend Program. The Chairman, Admission Committee, and the concerned Head of Department briefed the Worthy Vice Chancellor that the program is being introduced in response to the growing demand and persistent requests from prospective students. They highlighted that the program is expected to provide valuable opportunities for members of the local community, as well as professionals and individuals working in Skardu, enabling them to pursue higher education without interrupting their professional commitments.
The meeting was attended by Deans and Heads of Departments, who shared updates regarding academic activities and provided input on matters related to the ongoing and upcoming semesters.
The meeting concluded with a commitment to further strengthen academic coordination and ensure the smooth and timely execution of all academic activities at the University of Baltistan, Skardu.

Faculty of Natural Sciences and Technologies
Where scientific inquiry meets technological innovation to solve real-world problems.
Introduction
The Faculty of Natural Sciences and Technologies is a central academic unit of the University, dedicated to advancing knowledge in both fundamental and applied sciences. Our programs collectively foster a comprehensive understanding of the natural world and its technological applications.
The Faculty is committed to excellence in teaching, research, and innovation. Our academic programs provide a strong theoretical foundation, complemented by practical training and research opportunities, enabling students to develop analytical, problem-solving, and technical competencies.
Through an interdisciplinary and collaborative approach, the Faculty promotes intellectual inquiry and scientific discovery, addressing contemporary challenges in science, technology and the environment. We strive to produce graduates who are academically accomplished, ethically responsible, and well prepared for professional careers, advanced studies and research.
Our Mission
Our mission is to promote a deep understanding of the natural world through advanced research and scholarly inquiry; to provide high quality education that prepares students for leadership roles in science and technology; and to serve society through innovation, entrepreneurship and effective collaboration with community and industry partners.
Our Vision
To be a globally recognized center of excellence in scientific discovery and technological innovation, contributing to sustainable development and economic growth.
Key Focus Areas
Biotechnology & Genetic Engineering
Artificial Intelligence & Machine Learning
Data Science & Computational Modeling
Environmental & Sustainability Sciences
Molecular & Plant Sciences
Wildlife & Biodiversity Conservation
Chemical Analysis & Industrial Chemistry
Mathematical Modeling & Applied Mathematics
Software Development & Emerging Technologies
Dean's Message
Prof. Dr. Abdul Matin
Faculty of Natural Sciences and Technologies
Dean Since: 2021
It gives me great pleasure to welcome you to the Faculty of Natural Sciences and Technologies.
Our Faculty is dedicated to fostering a culture of academic excellence, innovation and integrity. We strive to create a dynamic learning environment where teaching, research and community engagement are closely integrated. By bringing together the disciplines of natural sciences and technology, we aim to equip our students with the knowledge and skills needed to address the complex challenges of the modern world.
Our faculty members are deeply committed to mentoring and inspiring students to think critically, explore new ideas, and uphold the highest ethical standards. We take pride in nurturing graduates who are not only academically competent but also socially responsible and capable of making meaningful contributions at both national and global levels.
Students remain at the heart of our mission. Through a balanced approach that combines strong theoretical foundations with research and practical experience, we prepare our graduates for successful careers, advanced studies and leadership roles in their respective fields.
As a Dean, I am proud of the vibrant academic community we continue to build. I warmly invite you to explore the opportunities our Faculty offers and to join us in our pursuit of knowledge, innovation and service to the society.

Seminar
3 min read
University of Baltistan Holds Seminar on “Serving Humanity: Patriotism Through Social Responsibility”
August 21, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for University of Baltistan Holds Seminar on “Serving Humanity: Patriotism Through Social Responsibility”
The University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS), organized a lecture on the theme “Serving Humanity: Patriotism Through Social Responsibility” as part of its monthly lecture series.
The keynote speaker was renowned historian, author, and researcher of Gilgit-Baltistan, Muhammad Hassan Hasrat. Dr. Sabir Ali, Lecturer in the Department of Educational Development,Naila Batool, Lecturer of Pakistan Studies, Social worker Yahya Jafri.
In a special message, Vice Chancellor Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar highlighted the importance of patriotism through community service and social responsibility. He stated that the rise and decline of nations is determined by selfless service to humanity, mutual tolerance, and meaningful participation in social life. The true purpose of higher education is not merely to confer degrees, but to instill national spirit, moral values, and a sense of social responsibility in students.
In his welcome address, Aftab Hussain highlighted the objectives of the lecture series and said that the decline of nations does not occur merely because of a lack of resources, but rather due to a lack of civic awareness, patriotism, and social responsibility.
Dr. Sabir Ali, Lecturer in the Department of Educational Development, also observed that nations decline not because of a shortage of resources but because of a lack of civic consciousness, patriotism, and social responsibility. Discussing the true meaning of patriotism, the broader concept of community, and environmental protection, he said that identifying and supporting the protectors of the homeland without prejudice, establishing bonds of love and cooperation with them, and standing alongside them are essential for national development.
Lecturer of Pakistan Studies, Naila Batool, stated that patriotism should not remain confined to slogans; its true expression lies in serving humanity. She noted that, given the region's challenging geographical conditions, educational and healthcare challenges, and environmental threats, young people must move beyond slogans and take practical action.
Renowned Youth social worker Yahya Jaffri said that we are all living as global citizens, and the real question is what solutions we can offer to address the problems faced by our communities. He also encouraged students to read two internationally recognized books to develop a deeper understanding of social issues.
The keynote speaker, renowned historian, author, and researcher Muhammad Hassan Hasrat, expressed his gratitude and appreciation to the administration of the University of Baltistan for organizing a seminar on such an important and relevant topic. He remarked that every time he visits the University of Baltistan, he gets an opportunity to learn something new. He further stated that education without proper training and character-building can become a double-edged sword that may cause harm rather than benefit.
At the conclusion, Dean, Faculty of Social Sciences, Prof. Dr. Ashfaque Ahmad Shah, thanked all the speakers and participants and presented a brief summary of the proceedings.
The programme began with the recitation of the Holy Quran by Muhammad Kamal, while Fozia performed the duties of moderator/host.

SKARDU
Office of Research, Innovation & Commercialization
Fostering Innovation, Driving Research Excellence, and Promoting Commercialization at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
About ORIC
Introduction
The Office of Research, Innovation & Commercialization (ORIC) at the University of Baltistan, Skardu functions as the focal point for coordinating, strengthening, and promoting research and innovation activities across the university. Established in alignment with the guidelines of the Higher Education Commission (HEC) of Pakistan, ORIC is dedicated to institutionalizing research governance, enhancing research quality, and fostering an enabling environment for scholarly excellence.
ORIC provides strategic direction for research planning, grant acquisition, industry linkages, and intellectual property management. It facilitates collaboration among faculty, students, industry partners, and national and international organizations to ensure impactful and solution-oriented research. The office also monitors funded projects, ensures compliance with regulatory frameworks, and promotes ethical research practices.
By supporting innovation, entrepreneurship, and commercialization initiatives, ORIC aims to translate academic research into practical applications that contribute to socio-economic development, particularly within Gilgit-Baltistan and beyond. Through capacity building and strategic partnerships, ORIC strives to position the university as a leading research-driven institution in the region.
Dr. Munawwar Ali Abbas
Director ORIC
"Research and innovation drive institutional growth and societal transformation. At ORIC, we are dedicated to fostering a culture of excellence, collaboration, and impact where ideas evolve into meaningful solutions for regional and national development."
oric@uobs.edu.pk
Our Strategic Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Our Mission
To strengthen research excellence, promote innovation, and support the commercialization of knowledge through effective governance, strategic collaborations, and capacity development. ORIC is committed to facilitating impactful research that addresses regional and national priorities, encourages interdisciplinary engagement, ensures ethical standards, and contributes to sustainable socio-economic development while enhancing the University of Baltistan's research visibility and competitiveness at national and international levels.
Our Vision
To emerge as a leading center of research, innovation, and commercialization in Gilgit-Baltistan, recognized nationally and internationally for excellence, integrity, and societal relevance. ORIC envisions fostering a vibrant research ecosystem that transforms knowledge into impactful solutions, strengthens industry and community linkages, promotes entrepreneurship, and contributes meaningfully to sustainable development, economic growth, and evidence-based policy advancement.
Key Objectives
•
Promote interdisciplinary research collaboration
•
Secure national and international funding
•
Strengthen industry–academia partnerships
•
Support innovation and commercialization
•
Ensure research quality and ethics
ORIC Team
Dedicated professionals supporting research excellence and innovation

Guidelines & Policies
Research Policies
Framework governing research activities at University of Baltistan
Research Ethics Policy
Framework ensuring ethical conduct in research involving human participants, animals, and sensitive data, aligned with national and international standards.
Intellectual Property (IP) Policy
Guidelines governing ownership, protection, patent filing, licensing, and commercialization of research outputs and innovations.
Research Grant Management Policy
Procedures for proposal submission, budget approval, fund utilization, financial reporting, and compliance monitoring of sponsored projects.
Publication and Authorship Policy
Standards for responsible authorship, publication ethics, plagiarism prevention, and adherence to open access and indexing requirements.
Research Integrity and Misconduct Policy
Mechanisms to address plagiarism, data fabrication, falsification, and other forms of academic misconduct.
Collaboration and MoU Policy
Guidelines for establishing, reviewing, and managing national and international research partnerships and institutional agreements.

Meeting
Featured
2 min read
Senate of UoBS Approves Key Governance, Academic and Financial Measures
September 10, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for Senate of UoBS Approves Key Governance, Academic and Financial Measures
The 13th meeting of the Senate of the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS), was held on Thursday September 10,2026 under the chairmanship of Mr. Afzal Ali Shigri, Chairman Senate. The meeting was attended by Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar, Vice Chancellor UoBS; Dr. Farkhanda Manzoor; Prof. Dr. Ishtiaq Ali, HEC Nominee; Mr. Agha Waseem Ahmed, Joint Secretary Ministry of Kashmir Affairs and Gilgit-Baltistan, Raja Rashid Ali, Secretary Higher Education, Gilgit-Baltistan; Mr. Mehr Dad; Mr. Muhammad Zaheer; Dr. Alamdar Hussain; Dr. Muhammad Issa; Mr. Nasir Abbas; and Dr. Mir Alam, Registrar as Secretary Senate. The Senate approved a range of measures relating to governance, academic restructuring, institutional development, affiliation, human resources and financial management. Key decisions included alignment of faculties and departments with the First Statutes and HEC requirements, strengthening of the Directorate of Advanced Studies and Research (DASR), approval of new and renewed affiliations to colleges, amendments to leave rules, and approval of the UoBS Adjunct Faculty Policy, 2026. The Senate also approved the University’s Actual Budget for FY 2024-25, Revised/Actual Budget for FY 2025-26 and Budget Estimates for FY 2026-27. The Annual Report 2025 was also presented. Chairman Senate Mr. Afzal Ali Shigri appreciated the University administration’s efforts towards institutional development and emphasized the timely implementation of the Senate’s decisions. Vice Chancellor Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar briefed the Senate on the University’s progress and future roadmap, with emphasis on academic excellence, research and institutional strengthening.

Office of the Project Director
Development Projects, Project Management, Monitoring, and Infrastructure Delivery
About the Project Director Office
Introduction
The Office of the Project Director at the University of Baltistan, Skardu is responsible for coordinating, supervising, and monitoring major university development projects. The office works closely with the University administration, Higher Education Commission (HEC), consultants, contractors, and other stakeholders to ensure that development projects are implemented efficiently and in accordance with approved plans and standards.
The Project Director Office focuses on project execution, progress monitoring, coordination with implementing agencies, quality control, documentation, and timely completion of approved development components. The office also facilitates project reviews and follows up on technical and administrative matters related to ongoing university infrastructure projects.
Dr. Zakir Hussain Zakir
Project Director
"Our aim is to ensure the timely, transparent, and quality implementation of the University of Baltistan development projects and to transform the approved vision into sustainable infrastructure that serves students, faculty, and the wider university community."
project.director@uobs.edu.pk
Project Direction
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Mission
To ensure effective planning, coordination, supervision, monitoring, and timely implementation of approved development projects of the University of Baltistan. The office is committed to quality construction, efficient project management, compliance with approved designs and specifications, and coordination with HEC, consultants, contractors, and relevant university authorities.
Vision
To deliver high-quality, sustainable, and resilient university infrastructure through effective project management, transparent coordination, timely execution, and continuous monitoring, contributing to a modern academic environment at the University of Baltistan, Skardu.
Objectives
Develop comprehensive short-term and long-term master plans for campus development and extensions
Secure funding for infrastructure and development projects from HEC, PSDP, and international donors
Monitor and ensure timely completion of all development projects within approved budgets
Implement sustainable and eco-friendly practices in all construction and development initiatives
Ensure optimal utilization of university land, buildings, and other resources
Promote sustainable practices and academic/research innovations
Project Director Office
Office responsible for coordination, supervision, monitoring, quality control, and implementation of university development projects
Sr.
Name
Designation
Role
Contact

Dr. Zakir Hussain Zakir

project.director@uobs.edu.pk

Seminar
1 min read
بلتستان یونیورسٹی سکردو میں حضور نبی کریمؐ کی ولادتِ باسعادت اور سیرتِ طیبہ کی ترویج و اشاعت کے لیے سلسلہ وار دروس و سیمینار کا اِنعقاد
August 28, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for بلتستان یونیورسٹی سکردو میں حضور نبی کریمؐ کی ولادتِ باسعادت اور سیرتِ طیبہ کی ترویج و اشاعت کے لیے سلسلہ وار دروس و سیمینار کا اِنعقاد
بلتستان یونیورسٹی میں سیرت النبیؐ پر سیمینار، نوجوانوں کو درپیش چیلنجز کا حل اُسوہ حسنہ میں پوشیدہ ہے، مقررین
بلتستان یونیورسٹی سکردو میں حضور نبی کریمؐ کی ولادتِ باسعادت اور سیرتِ طیبہ کی ترویج و اشاعت کے لیے ایک سلسلہ وار دروس و سیمینار کا اِنعقاد کیا گیا۔ سیمینار کا عنوان ”اُسوہ حسنہ کی روشنی میں نوجوانوں کی ذمہ داریوں کا تعین“تھا۔ سیمینار میں تلاوت کا شرف شعبہ باٹنی کے طالب علم حافظ نوازش علی کو حاصل ہوا جبکہ نعت ِ رسول مقبولؐ اِسی شعبہ کی ہی طالبہ سیدہ زہرا نے پڑھی۔ سیمینار کے حوالے سے اپنے خصوصی پیغام میں وائس چانسلر پروفیسر ڈاکٹر مسعود اختر نے کہا کہ نبی کریمؐ کی ذاتِ گرامی کائنات کے لیے ہدایت اور عظیم الشان نمونہ ہے۔ انہوں نے اس بات پر زور دیا کہ موجودہ دور میں نوجوانوں کو درپیش چیلنجز سے نبرد آزما ہونے کے لیے سیرتِ طیبہ کا مطالعہ اور اس پر عملی پیرا ہونا وقت کی اہم ترین ضرورت ہے تاکہ نوجوان اپنی زندگیوں کو اُسوہ حسنہ کے سانچے میں ڈھال کر معاشرے میں مثبت، علمی اور اخلاقی انقلاب برپا کر سکیں۔ سیمینار میں مشہور و معروف عالمِ دین سجاد حسین مفتیؔ نے اپنے بصیرت افروز اور فکری خطاب میں کہا کہ استاد خود نہیں بولتا بلکہ وہ آدھا جملہ بولتا ہے اور آدھا طالب علم سے سنتا ہے، انہوں نے واضح کیا کہ حضور نبی کریمؐ اپنی ذاتی خواہش سے گفتگو نہیں کرتے بلکہ تمام اُمور میں اللہ تعالیٰ کی وحی کے تابع ہوتے ہیں۔ سجاد حسین مفتیؔ نے سیرتِ طیبہ کے حوالے سے قرآن مجید کی تین آیات کا حوالہ دیتے ہوئے کہا کہ رحمت محض انسان کی بنیادی ضرورت کا نام نہیں بلکہ رسول اللہؐ کی شخصیت عقل، فکر، دل، جذبات، گفتار اور کردار جیسے اعلیٰ ترین اور کامل صفات کا مجموعہ ہے اور ہم سب قرآن، حدیث اور اپنے بزرگوں کے نقوشِ قدم سے سیکھتے ہوئے ہی آج حضورؐ سے ہدایت حاصل کرنے یہاں جمع ہوئے ہیں۔ انہوں نے انسانی ساخت پر روشنی ڈالتے ہوئے کہا کہ ہر چیز کی دو جہتیں ہوتی ہیں یعنی ایک ظاہری مادی جسم اور دوسری باطنی روح جو قوتِ محرکہ، نافذہ اور ارادے کی حامل ہوتی ہے۔ نبی کریمؐ مادی جسم کے اعتبار سے ظاہری طور پر ہمارے جیسے انسان ہیں لیکن روح کے اعتبار سے ہم میں اور آپؐ میں عظیم فرق موجود ہے کیونکہ ہماری روح میں نورانیت کی کمی ہے جبکہ حضورؐ کی روحِ مبارک نورانیت کا سرچشمہ ہے۔ سجاد حسین مفتیؔ نے مسلم اُمت کے موجودہ حال کا تجزیہ کرتے ہوئے کہا کہ ہم بحیثیت مسلمان نبی کریمؐ پر کامل ایمان رکھتے ہیں مگر ہمیں یہ بات سمجھنی ہوگی کہ مغرب کی ترقی کا راز ترکِ مذہب تھا جبکہ مسلمانوں کی پستی کی بڑی وجہ بھی ترکِ مذہب ہی بنی، مغرب نے مذہب سے دوری اختیار کر کے دنیاوی ترقی حاصل کر لی مگر مسلمان مذہب کو چھوڑ کر بھی ترقی کی راہ پر گامزن نہ ہو سکے۔ انہوں نے علم کی اہمیت پر زور دیتے ہوئے یاد دہانی کرائی کہ حضرت آدم ؑ کو بھی سجدہ ان کے علمی تفوق کی بنیاد پر ہوا تھا اور نبی کریمؐ نے مکے کی زمین سے علم و بصیرت کی روشنی پھیلائی۔انہوں نے مزید کہا کہ دینِ محمدیؐ دراصل تعلیم، تحقیق، قرآن، مشاہدہ، تطبیق، تسخیر اور تخلیق کا مجموعہ ہے اور جب کوئی فرد ان تمام اعلیٰ صفات سے آراستہ ہوتا ہے تو وہی حقیقت میں عالم کے درجے پر فائز ہوتا ہے۔ اپنے خطاب کے اختتام پر انہوں نے تاریخی اور فکری تسلسل کو واضح کرتے ہوئے کہا کہ ہمارا ماضی بعثتِ نبویؐ سے منسلک ہے، ہمارا مستقبل محمدی انقلاب سے مہدوی حکومت تک پر محیط ہے، جبکہ ہمارا حال غلامی اور جمود کا شکار ہے کیونکہ ہم نبی کریمؐ سے عقیدت و محبت کا دعویٰ تو کرتے ہیں لیکن ان کے لائے ہوئے مکمل نظامِ زندگی کو عملی طور پر قبول کرنے کے لیے تیار نہیں ہیں۔ قبل ازیں سیمینار کی شروعات میں مشیر امورِ طلبہ بلتستان یونیورسٹی ڈاکٹر محمد ریاض نے استقبالیہ کلمات ادا کرتے ہوئے معزز مہمان گرامی اور تمام شرکاء کو خوش آمدید کہا۔ انہوں نے سلسلہ ہائے دروس کے اِنعقاد کے اغراض و مقاصد پر تفصیلی گفتگو کرتے ہوئے کہا کہ اس سلسلہ وار پروگرام کا بنیادی مقصد طلبہ و طالبات اور جوانوں میں سیرتِ طیبہ کی فکری و عملی ترویج، اخلاقی تربیّت اور ان میں اسلامی و معاشرتی ذمہ داریوں کا احساس بیدار کرنا ہے۔واضح رہے کہ سیمینار کے اِنتظام و انصرام کی ذمہ داری دفترمشیر اُمورِ طلبہ اور علومِ اسلامی کے اساتذہ نے مشترکہ طور پر نبھائی جبکہ شرکائے سیمینار میں مختلف شعبہ جات کے طلباء وطالبات شامل تھے۔

Powers and Duties of the Academic Council
The Academic Council is the principal academic body of the University, responsible for promoting and coordinating teaching, research, and academic excellence in accordance with the UoBS Order, 2016.
Formulate and regulate academic policies and programs
Approve curricula, syllabi, and courses of study
Promote research and coordinate research activities
Recommend standards for admission to the University
Regulate examinations and approve results
Recommend conferment of degrees, diplomas, and certificates
Determine academic calendar and schedule
Consider academic collaborations and linkages
Promote innovation in teaching and learning methods
Advise on academic standards and quality assurance
Recommend academic appointments and promotions
Guide academic planning and development

Event
Past Event
University of Baltistan Observes Youm-e-Istehsal Kashmir, Reaffirms Solidarity with the People of Jammu and Kashmir
Date
Aug 5, 2026
Venue
University of Baltistan Skardu
Event Overview
The University of Baltistan, Skardu (UOBS) observed Youm-e-Istehsal Kashmir with a commemorative programme to express solidarity with the people of Indian Illegally Occupied Jammu and Kashmir (IIOJK) and to reaffirm support for their internationally recognized right to self-determination.
The event was attended by members of the University's administration, faculty, staff, and students. Participants expressed solidarity with the people of Jammu and Kashmir and reiterated their support for the peaceful pursuit of their fundamental rights. The programme also highlighted the importance of raising awareness of the humanitarian and human rights dimensions of the Kashmir issue.
In his message on the occasion, Vice Chancellor Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar stated that organizing programmes on important national issues is part of the University's academic, civic, and moral responsibility.
"Universities have an important role in promoting informed dialogue, civic awareness, and responsible engagement with issues of national and international significance. We remain committed to providing platforms for constructive discussion and to expressing solidarity with the people of Jammu and Kashmir in accordance with Pakistan's principled position on their right to self-determination," he said.
Addressing the participants, Registrar Dr. Mir Alam emphasized the University's continued moral and diplomatic support for the people of Jammu and Kashmir. He called upon the international community to pay greater attention to reported human rights concerns and urged renewed efforts toward a peaceful and just resolution of the Kashmir dispute in accordance with relevant United Nations Security Council resolutions.
Speaking on the occasion, Lecturer Aftab highlighted the role of young people and educational institutions in promoting awareness of global issues. He encouraged students to engage with international affairs through informed academic discourse, research, and responsible advocacy, emphasizing that universities serve as important forums for fostering critical thinking and civic responsibility.
The programme concluded with a collective call for the international community to support efforts aimed at ensuring that the people of Jammu and Kashmir are able to exercise their right to self-determination through peaceful means in accordance with relevant United Nations resolutions.
The University of Baltistan reaffirmed its commitment to promoting informed academic dialogue, civic responsibility, peace, and respect for human rights through educational activities and public engagement.

Student Health Services
First Aid Center | On-Campus Medical Support for Students
First Aid Center
The University First Aid Center provides essential on-campus medical support to all enrolled students and staff during campus hours. Our facility maintains a well-stocked medicine cabinet and is equipped to handle common cases such as headaches, colds, gastrointestinal issues, minor injuries, and initial response for hypertension (HTN) or road traffic accidents (RTA).
We offer
separate first-aid facilities for male and female students
with privacy curtains to ensure comfort and confidentiality. Our services include basic medical assessments, first-aid treatment and bandaging, IV line setup, medicine distribution, and emergency referral coordination with RHQ Hospital and Rescue 1122.
Available Services:
Basic medical consultations & assessments
First-aid treatment, bandaging & IV line setup
Temperature & blood pressure monitoring
Medicine distribution from well-stocked cabinet
Emergency initial response & referral coordination
Separate male/female sections with privacy curtains
First Aid Center
Medical Room & Resting Area | Ground Floor, Academic Block 2
Separate Male/Female Sections Available
Location:
Ground Floor, Academic Block 2

Software Engineer at IT Company in Solvokia
Mr. Asghar Abbas
BS Computer Science 2013-2017
Computer Engineer
Solvokia
Success Story
I graduated in 2017 from Karakoram International University (KIU), Skardu Campus — now proudly known as the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UOBS). My time at the university played a transformative role in shaping both my technical foundation and professional mindset.
I remain deeply grateful to my mentor, the late Dr. Irshad Ali, whose guidance, wisdom, and encouragement had a lasting impact on my life. He believed in nurturing not just academic excellence but also confidence and character in his students. His mentorship helped me think beyond limitations and strive for continuous growth. I also sincerely appreciate all my respected teachers whose dedication, knowledge, and support built the strong foundation upon which my career stands today.
Shortly after graduation, I moved to Karachi to pursue my career in the IT industry. I began my professional journey at Sator IT as a .NET intern. Through persistence, continuous learning, and hard work, I steadily progressed within the organization. Over the years, I became one of the most senior and trusted team members. In August 2025, I resigned while serving as a Senior Software Architect — a journey that began from an internship and evolved into a leadership role.
Alongside my professional development, I completed my Master’s degree in Software Engineering in 2021, further strengthening my technical expertise and leadership capabilities.
In September 2025, I moved to Slovakia after receiving a job offer from a new IT company, marking an important international milestone in my career.
My journey from KIU Skardu Campus (now UOBS) to working internationally reflects the power of education, mentorship, and determination. I hope my story inspires students to believe in themselves, remain resilient, and never stop learning.
About This Achievement
Mr. Asghar Abbas achieved
software engineer at it company in solvokia while studying
BS Computer Science 2013-2017.
This remarkable accomplishment demonstrates the quality of education and opportunities available at University of Baltistan.
Academic Program

Campus Security
Ensuring a Safe and Secure Learning Environment
24/7 Security Presence
Our trained security personnel are always on duty
Professional Security Team
Our campus is protected by a professional security team trained in emergency response,
crowd management, and crisis intervention. Security personnel are stationed at all entry
points and patrol the campus 24 hours a day, 7 days a week.
24/7 Security patrols across campus
Trained first responders and emergency staff
Quick response to any security situation
Emergency: Ext. 999 | Security Control: Ext. 900
Complete CCTV Coverage
Our campus is monitored by advanced surveillance systems ensuring safety at all times
250+
High-Definition Cameras
100%
Campus Coverage
24/7
Real-Time Monitoring
All entrances, academic buildings, parking areas, and common spaces under surveillance
Student Affairs Office
For any security concerns, complaints, or issues, please contact the Student Affairs Office.
We are here to help and ensure your concerns are addressed promptly.
Location
Admin Block, Ground Floor
Office Hours
Monday - Friday: 9:00 AM - 5:00 PM

Fall 2026 Admission Timeline
Extension of Admission Deadline & Revised Admission Schedule – Fall 2026
This is for the information of all prospective applicants that, in view of the internet disruption caused by the recent flood situation in different parts of the region,
the last date for submission of online applications for Admission Fall 2026 has been extended up to Friday, August 07, 2026, to facilitate applicants who were
unable to complete their applications within the previous deadline.
The revised admission schedule is as follows:

July 23, 2026
Admission notices published in newspapers and on university website

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Won MS Scholarship(South Korea)
Mr. Muhammad Waseem
BS Computer Science2017-2021
Computer Science
South Korea
Research
Success Story
I graduated from the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UOBS) in 2017, and my journey since then has been driven by a deep passion for research and innovation in Machine Learning, Deep Learning and Graphically Surgical Simulation.
My time at UOBS played a fundamental role in shaping my academic foundation, critical thinking abilities, and determination to pursue higher education.
The supportive academic environment at UOBS encouraged me to explore complex problem-solving and develop a strong interest in computational methods and graphics-based simulations. The knowledge and confidence I gained there motivated me to continue my academic journey internationally.
Currently, I am pursuing my PhD at Soonchunhyang University, South Korea, in the Computer Graphics Lab.
My research focuses on advanced particle-based simulations, Fluid Dynamics, Bone Healing Simulation and real-time visualization techniques. I was honored to receive the Best Conference Paper Award at an IEEE international conference held in Malaysia. I also successfully published a Q1-ranked research paper, marking an important milestone in my research career.
These achievements reflect not only personal dedication but also the strong educational foundation built during my undergraduate studies at UOBS. I remain grateful to my teachers and mentors who encouraged me to aim higher and pursue excellence.
About This Achievement
Mr. Muhammad Waseem achieved
won ms scholarship(south korea) while studying
BS Computer Science2017-2021.
This remarkable accomplishment demonstrates the quality of education and opportunities available at University of Baltistan.

Past Event
جشنِ آزادی محض پرچم لہرانے کا نام نہیں، عملی جدوجہد، سخت محنت اور غیر متزلزل عزم کا تقاضا کرتا ہے، وائس چانسلر پروفیسر ڈاکٹر مسعود اختر
Date
Aug 14, 2026
Venue
To be announced
Event Overview
بلتستان یونیورسٹی سکردو میں یومِ آزادیِ پاکستان کی دو روزہ سرگرمیاں اختتام پذیر، وائس چانسلر نے تعلیمی پیش رفت اور ملک و قوم کی عملی خدمت پر زور دیا
سکردو (14 اگست 2026ء) بلتستان یونیورسٹی سکردو کے زیرِ اہتمام یومِ آزادیِ پاکستان کی مناسبت سے مُنعقدہ دو روزہ سرگرمیاں اپنے تمام تر جوش و خروش، قومی جذبے اور شائقین کی بھرپور شرکت کے ساتھ اختتام پذیر ہو گئیں۔ دو روزہ سرگرمیوں کا باقاعدہ آغاز 13 اگست کو مختلف طلبہ مقابلوں سے ہوا جبکہ 14 اگست کی صبح یونیورسٹی کے چبوترے پر پرچم کشائی کی تقریب منعقد ہوئی۔ قومی ترانے کی گونج میں سرسبز و ہلال پرچم لہرایا گیا اور ملک کی سلامتی، استحکام اور ترقی کے لئے خصوصی دعائیں مانگی گئیں۔ اس پر موقع پر جشنِ آزادی کا کیک بھی کاٹا گیا۔ تقریب میں وائس چانسلر پروفیسر ڈاکٹر مسعود اختر، رئیسِ کلیہ سماجی علوم پروفیسر ڈاکٹر اشفاق احمد شاہ، رئیسِ کلیہ نیچرل سائنسز پروفیسر ڈاکٹر عبدالمتین، پرنسپل آفیسرز، شعبہ جات کے صدور، اساتذہ کرام اور طلباء و طالبات کی ایک بڑی تعداد نے شرکت کی اور قومی یکجہتی کا بھرپور مظاہرہ کیا۔ اس موقع پر تقریب سے خطاب کرتے ہوئے وائس چانسلر پروفیسر ڈاکٹر مسعود اختر نے اساتذہ اور طلبہ کے جذبے کو سراہتے ہوئے کہا کہ جشنِ آزادی محض ایک رواجی تہوار یا صرف پرچم لہرانے کا نام نہیں بلکہ یہ دن ہم سے عملی جدوجہد، سخت محنت اور غیر متزلزل عزم کا تقاضا کرتا ہے۔ انہوں نے کہا کہ ملک و قوم کی ترقی کا راز دیانت داری، اعلیٰ تعلیم اور مثبت سوچ میں پنہاں ہے اور ہمیں علم کی شمع روشن کرتے ہوئے پوری سچائی کے ساتھ وطنِ عزیز کی خدمت کرنی ہوگی۔ وائس چانسلر نے ارضِ پاک کے امن اور دفاع کے لئے سیکورٹی اداروں اور شہداء کی بے مثال قربانیوں کو زبردست خراجِ تحسین پیش کیا اور اس عزم کا اعادہ کیا کہ یونیورسٹی ان کی قربانیوں کی روشنی میں نئی نسل کی تعلیم و تربیت کے لئے اپنا موثر کردار ادا کرتی رہے گی۔ انہوں نے یونیورسٹی کی تعلیمی خدمات کو وسعت دینے کے عزم کا اظہار کرتے ہوئے اعلان کیا کہ بہت جلد یونیورسٹی کے تحت ایک جدید ماڈل اسکول قائم کیا جائے گا جو علاقے کے بچوں کو معیاری اور جدید ترین تعلیمی سہولیات فراہم کرے گا۔ پرچم کشائی کی تقریب کے بعد دو روزہ مقابلوں کے فائنل راؤنڈز کا انعقاد کیا گیا جس میں طلباء و طالبات نے اپنی خداداد صلاحیتوں کا مظاہرہ کیا۔ اُردو تقریری مقابلے کے سنسنی خیز فائنل میں شعبہ کمپیوٹر سائنس کی نہال علی اور شعبہ ایجوکیشنل ڈیولپمنٹ کی طاہرہ بتول نے مشترکہ طور پر پہلی جبکہ شعبہ لینگویجز اینڈ کلچرل اسٹڈیز کی علیزہ نے دوسری پوزیشن حاصل کی۔ انگریزی تقریری مقابلے میں شعبہ لینگویجز کی رضیہ حامد نے پہلی اور فوزیہ نے دوسری پوزیشن اپنے نام کی۔ فنِ مصوری کے مقابلے میں شعبہ ایجوکیشنل ڈیولپمنٹ کی ثریا نے اپنی بہترین تخلیقی صلاحیتوں کے بل بوتے پر پہلی پوزیشن حاصل کی جبکہ ملی نغموں کے مقابلے میں شعبہ زولوجی کے حسن نے روح پرور پیشکش پر پہلی اور یوشیع نے دوسری پوزیشن حاصل کی۔ کوئز کے کانٹے دار مقابلے میں شعبہ لینگویجز اینڈ کلچرل اسٹڈیز کی ٹیم ۔بی نے نمایاں کارکردگی کا مظاہرہ کرتے ہوئے فائنل ٹرافی اپنے نام کی۔ تقاریب کے اختتامی مرحلے میں اسناد تقسیم کرنے کی پروقار تقریب منعقد ہوئی۔تقریب کے مہمانِ خصوصی وائس چانسلر پروفیسر ڈاکٹر مسعود اختر تھے۔ انہوں نے مختلف مقابلوں، فنِ مصوری، کوئز، تقریری مقابلوں اور ملی نغموں میں نمایاں پوزیشنیں حاصل کرنے والے طلبہ اور فاتح ٹیموں میں اسناد اور انعامات تقسیم کیے اور ان کی شاندار کاوشوں کو سراہتے ہوئے ان کے روشن مستقبل کے لیے نیک خواہشات کا اظہار کیا۔ علاوہ ازیں صدر شعبہ باٹنی ڈاکٹر اشتیاق حسین منڈوقؔ نے گزشتہ دنوں یونیورسٹی میں منعقدہ 10 روزہ ’معرکہ حق‘ کی سرگرمیوں میں نمایاں کارکردگی دکھانے والےشعبہ باٹنی کے طلباء وطالبات کو وائس چانسلر کے ہاتھوں شیلڈز تقسیم کئے جبکہ معرکہ حق کے کنوینئر ڈاکٹر محمد ریاض کو بھی ان کی بہترین خدمات کے اعتراف میں شیلڈ سے نوازا گیا۔

UNIVERSITY OF BALTISTAN, SKARDU
PARKING FACILITIES
Comprehensive parking solutions for students, faculty, staff, and visitors at the University of Baltistan, Skardu.
Our secure, organized parking facilities ensure convenient access while maintaining campus safety and traffic flow.
About Our Parking Facilities
The University of Baltistan, Skardu provides comprehensive parking facilities for students, faculty, staff, and visitors. Our parking system ensures safe, organized, and convenient parking with designated zones for different user groups and strict security measures.
Our Mission
To provide secure, accessible, and well-organized parking solutions that support the university community while maintaining campus safety and traffic flow.
Our Vision
To implement smart parking solutions and sustainable transportation management for an efficient campus environment.
Operating Hours
Parking Availability
24/7
Security Office
Monday-Friday, 9:00 AM - 5:00 PM
Permit Office
Monday-Friday, 9:00 AM - 5:00 PM
Note:
Parking permits are required for most zones. Visitors must use designated visitor parking areas.

UoBS Faculty Member Secures International Award for World Meningitis Day 2026
August 31, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for UoBS Faculty Member Secures International Award for World Meningitis Day 2026
Skardu, Pakistan: Prof. Dr. Abdul Matin, Dean, Faculty of Natural
Sciences & Technologies, University of Baltistan, Skardu, has secured an
international award under the World Meningitis Day 2026 Advocacy Fund for his
project entitled “Youth Against Meningitis: Awareness, Advocacy and Action for
World Meningitis Day 2026 in Gilgit-Baltistan, Pakistan.”
The project was selected through a competitive international review process
supported by the Meningitis Research Foundation (MRF), United Kingdom, through
the Confederation of Meningitis Organisations (CoMO), and is one of six
projects funded globally under the World Meningitis Day 2026 Advocacy Fund.
The three-month initiative, being implemented during August–October
2026, will focus on strengthening meningitis awareness and advocacy among young
people and communities in Gilgit-Baltistan. Planned activities include student
engagement, awareness sessions, educational outreach, advocacy activities,
social-media engagement and activities marking World Meningitis Day on 5
October 2026.
As part of the initiative, 20 student volunteers will be selected and
trained as World Meningitis Ambassadors to support meningitis awareness and
outreach activities among students and in local communities.
The University of Baltistan, Skardu will provide an important academic
platform for the implementation of major project activities, particularly youth
engagement, awareness and ambassador training. Faculty members and students
will have opportunities to participate in the initiative.
The project represents an important opportunity to promote
public-health awareness, youth leadership and community engagement while
strengthening the University's links with national and international
public-health networks. It also reflects the University's commitment to
interdisciplinary engagement in areas including public health, microbiology,
environmental health and One Health.
The initiative will be implemented in collaboration with relevant
educational institutions, health professionals, community stakeholders and
other partners in Gilgit-Baltistan.
The project is expected to contribute to greater awareness of
meningitis among young people and communities while creating opportunities for
future collaboration, research, capacity building and evidence-based advocacy
related to meningitis prevention and public health.

Roles & Functions of Faculty Councils
Faculty Councils are academic bodies responsible for coordinating and supervising teaching, research, and examination activities within their respective faculties. They ensure academic standards and facilitate inter-departmental collaboration.
Recommend curriculum development and revisions for undergraduate and postgraduate programs
Approve course contents, syllabi, and reading materials for all programs
Coordinate and promote research activities within the faculty
Oversee student admissions, examinations, and academic progression
Recommend faculty appointments, promotions, and development programs
Prepare academic calendar and schedule of examinations
Facilitate collaboration between departments within the faculty
Monitor academic standards and quality assurance measures
Approve research proposals and thesis topics for postgraduate students
Recommend conferment of degrees to the Academic Council
Address student grievances related to academic matters
Coordinate with other faculties and academic bodies of the university

Powers and Duties of the Senate
The Senate shall be the supreme governing body of the University and shall, subject to the provisions of the UoBS Order, 2016, exercise general supervision over the affairs and management of the University.
Take effective measures to raise the standard of teaching, research and publication
Hold control and administer the property and funds of the University
Govern and regulate the finances, accounts and investments of the University
Consider and pass the annual report, statements of accounts, and budget estimates
Enter into, vary, carry out and cancel contracts on behalf of the University
Approve Statutes and Regulations for the governance of the University
Institute academic and administrative posts, and appoint personnel
Confer Honorary Degrees and approve academic programs
Establish Faculties, Teaching Departments, Centers and Institutes
Determine the form and regulate the use of the Common Seal of the University
Provide buildings, libraries, equipment and other means required for University work
Establish and maintain halls of residence and hostels for students
View Complete UoBS Order, 2016

Department of Chemistry
The Department of Chemistry at the University of Baltistan, Skardu is dedicated to excellence in chemical education and research.
Established: 2018
HEC Recognized
144+ Students
Introduction
The Department of Chemistry at the University of Baltistan, Skardu is dedicated to excellence in chemical education and research. Since its establishment in 2018, the department has been committed to producing competent graduates with strong theoretical and practical knowledge in various branches of chemistry.
Department Statistics
144+
Total Students
4
Faculty Members
2
Academic Programs
3
Laboratories
5
Active Batches
Gender Distribution
29%
Male (42)
71%
Female (102)
Vision & Mission
Our Vision
The Department of Chemistry aspires to be a nationally recognized model for educating and graduating students who are prepared to compete in and contribute to an ever-changing world. To realize this vision, the department is committed to offering undergraduates a course of study in the chemical sciences that integrates rigorous curriculum, meaningful scholarship, and hands-on service and engagement opportunities. These experiences will be high-quality, innovative, intellectually challenging, and enhanced by state-of-the-art technologies.
Our Mission
To improve people's quality of life by harnessing the innovative and transformative power of the chemical sciences, shaping graduates into entrepreneurs who respond to society's needs.
We develop a deep understanding of chemistry through superior teaching, hands-on laboratory activities, mini-research projects, and advocacy—all under the guidance of highly qualified faculty—to prepare individuals for the evolving revolution in the chemical sciences.
Core Values
Academic Integrity
Research Excellence
Innovation
Teamwork

Seminar
1 min read
بلتستان یونیورسٹی میں ”حفاظتِ ماحول اور نبوی ہدایات“ کے عنوان سے سلسلہ ہائے دروس کا تیسرا سیشن
September 4, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for بلتستان یونیورسٹی میں ”حفاظتِ ماحول اور نبوی ہدایات“ کے عنوان سے سلسلہ ہائے دروس کا تیسرا سیشن
سکردو (مورخہ 4 ستمبر 2026ء) بلتستان یونیورسٹی سکردو میں دفتر ایس اے (اسٹوڈنٹ ایڈوائزر) کے زیرِ اہتمام اور بمناسبت ولادت باسعادت سرکارِ دو عالم حضرت محمد ﷺ سلسلہ ہائے دروس کا تیسرا سیشن منعقد کیا گیا۔”حفاظتِ ماحول اور نبوی ہدایات: حیاتیاتی تنوع اور قدرتی وسائل کا استعمال“ کے عنوان سے منعقدہ تیسرے سیشن میں عالمی شہرت یافتہ مذہبی اسکالر ڈاکٹر معصومہ جعفری نے بطور مہمانِ مقرر خطاب کیا جبکہ سیشن میں اساتذہ اور طلباء و طالبات کی ایک بڑی تعداد موجود تھی۔ اس موقع پر وائس چانسلر پروفیسر ڈاکٹر مسعود اختر نے اپنے پیغام میں کہا کہ سیرتِ طیبہﷺ ہمیں فطرت کا احترام، ماحول کی صفائی اور قدرتی وسائل کا مخلصانہ و متوازن استعمال سیکھاتی ہے۔ انہوں نے واضح کیا کہ حیاتیاتی تنوع اور درختوں و پانی کا تحفظ دراصل سیرتِ پیغمبر اسلام ﷺ پر عمل پیرا ہونے کا بہترین عملی ذریعہ ہے، لہٰذا میں یونیورسٹی کے اساتذہ اور طلبہ کو دعوت دیتا ہوں کہ وہ سیرتِ نبوی ﷺ کی روشنی میں ماحول کو سرسبز اور متوازن بنانے میں اپنا فعال کردار ادا کریں۔ اسٹوڈنٹ ایڈوائزر ڈاکٹر محمد ریاض نے سیمینار کے اغراض و مقاصد پر روشنی ڈالتے ہوئے کہا کہ سلسلہ وار دروس کا مقصد نوجوانوں میں یہ احساس بیدار کرنا ہے کہ ماحول کی حفاظت صرف ایک شہری ذمہ داری نہیں بلکہ اہم دینی و اخلاقی فریضہ اور سنتِ رسول اللہ ﷺ بھی ہے۔ ان کا مزید کہنا تھا کہ دنیا بھر میں درپیش ماحولیاتی بحران اور حیاتیاتی تنوع کے نقصان کا حل سیرتِ طیبہ ﷺ کی روشنی میں ہی ممکن ہے تاکہ لوگ اسلامی تعلیمات کے ذریعے ماحول سے محبت اور اس کی پاسداری سیکھ سکیں۔ مہمانِ خصوصی عالمی شہرت یافتہ مذہبی اسکالر ڈاکٹر معصومہ جعفری نے اپنے خطاب کے آغاز میں سورۂ رحمن کی آیاتِ مبارکہ کی تلاوت کرتے ہوئے خطہ گلگت بلتستان کی سلامتی، قدرتی وسائل کے تحفظ اور یہاں کے باسیوں کی عاقبت بالخیر کے لیے دعائیہ کلمات کہے۔ انہوں نے بلتستان یونیورسٹی کے تعلیمی ماحول، خوبصورت عمارت اور نوجوانوں کے علمی جذبے کی تعریف کرتے ہوئے یونیورسٹی اِنتظامیہ کا شکریہ ادا کیا۔ ان کا کہنا تھا کہ اللہ تعالیٰ نے انسانیت کی تخلیق سے پہلے ہی ان کی تمام ضروریات کو مدنظر رکھتے ہوئے یہ قدرتی ماحول اور کائنات کا توازن قائم کیا اور قرآنِ مجید کی روشنی میں انسان اس زمین پر صرف نفع اُٹھانے والا نہیں بلکہ قدرت کی تمام نعمتوں جیسے پانی، ہوا، درخت اور پہاڑوں کا امین اور خلیفہ ہے۔ انہوں نے افسوس کا اظہار کرتے ہوئے کہا کہ انسان نے جب اپنے مفاد اور لالچ کے لیے قدرت کے نظام میں مداخلت کی تو زمین و فضا میں فساد برپا ہوا جس کے نتیجے میں آج فضائی و آبی آلودگی اور گلگت بلتستان سمیت دنیا بھر میں سیلاب اور فطری وسائل کی کمی جیسے شدید بحران جنم لے رہے ہیں۔ انہوں نے بتایا کہ قرآن کریم میں تقریباً 750 آیات ایسی ہیں جو براہِ راست یا بالواسطہ نیچر اور قدرتی وسائل کا احاطہ کرتی ہیں جبکہ متعدد سورتوں کے نام بھی حیوانات اور فطرت کے مظاہر پر رکھے گئے ہیں۔ فرمانِ نبوی ﷺ کا حوالہ دیتے ہوئے انہوں نے کہا کہ اگر قیامت قائم ہو رہی ہو اور تم میں سے کسی کے ہاتھ میں پودے کی ایک چھوٹی سی شاخ ہو تو اسے چاہیے کہ قیامت سے پہلے اس پودے کو زمین میں لگا دے۔ انہوں نے زور دیا کہ ماحولیات کا تحفظ صرف ایک سائنسی ضرورت نہیں بلکہ ہر مسلمان کی اخلاقی اور دینی ذمہ داری ہے اور تمام طلبہ و اساتذہ اپنے خطے کی قدرتی پاکیزگی کو برقرار رکھنے کے لیے عملی اقدامات کریں۔سیشن کے آغاز میں تلاوتِ کلامِ پاک کی سعادت شعبہ باٹنی کے طالب علم حافظ نوازش علی نے حاصل کی جبکہ نعتِ رسول مقبول ﷺ پیش کرنے کا شرف شعبہ اُردو کی طالبہ قرۃ العین کو حاصل ہوا۔

Why Choose University of Baltistan (UoBS)?
A Public Sector University Committed to Quality Education
University of Baltistan (UoBS) is dedicated to academic excellence, affordability, research, and holistic student development in the heart of Gilgit-Baltistan.
500+
Students Supported Annually
PKR 50M+
Scholarship Amount
15+
Scholarship Programs
2000+
Active Students
Why UoBS Stands Out
Academic Excellence
UoBS is committed to delivering high-quality education through experienced faculty, updated curricula, and outcome-based learning that prepares students for real-world success.
Accessible & Affordable Education
We believe that financial limitations should never restrict talent. UoBS offers quality education at affordable fees to ensure equal opportunities for all.
Modern Learning Facilities
Our campus features well-equipped classrooms, digital libraries, science labs, and IT infrastructure designed to support innovation and hands-on learning.
A Campus Like No Other
Located in the heart of the Karakoram, UoBS offers a peaceful, inspiring, and naturally beautiful environment that enhances both learning and personal growth.
Career-Oriented Programs
Our academic programs are designed to build practical skills and professional confidence, opening doors to diverse career opportunities in Pakistan and abroad.
Research & Innovation Focus
UoBS promotes a strong research culture that encourages critical inquiry, creativity, and solutions to regional and global challenges.
Financial Support
Scholarship Opportunities
UoBS offers various scholarship programs to support deserving and talented students. Financial constraints should never be a barrier to quality education.
Merit-Based Scholarships
For students with exceptional academic performance (85%+ marks). Covers up to 100% tuition fee.
Need-Based Scholarships
Financial assistance for students from low-income families (income below PKR 40,000/month).
Marafie Scholarships
Funded by Marafie Foundation for outstanding students from Gilgit-Baltistan region.
Honahar Scholarships
Government of Pakistan scholarship program for talented students from remote areas.
Kinship Scholarships
Special scholarships for children of university employees and armed forces personnel.
Explore All Scholarships

Office of the Registrar
The backbone of university administration - guardian and custodian of academic records, bridging academic systems with support services since inception
About Registrar Office
The Registrar Office is the backbone of University of Baltistan, Skardu, playing a vital role in facilitating students from enrollment through graduation. As the guardian and custodian of all students' academic and personal data, we partner with students, faculty members & staff to promote excellence through curriculum innovation, community-building, secure and accurate academic information, and a robust registration infrastructure.
We are the
BRIDGE
between the academic system and the allied academic support system of the university. The Office is committed primarily to the service of faculty and students by assisting in the interpretation and application of regulations, rules, and guidelines.
Dr. Mir Alam
Registrar
•
PhD

SKARDU
Quality Enhancement Cell
Ensuring academic excellence and continuous improvement at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
About QEC
Introduction
The Directorate of Quality Enhancement Cell (QEC) at the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS), serves as the central regulatory body dedicated to maintaining and elevating the standards of higher education. Established under the guidelines of the Higher Education Commission (HEC) Pakistan, the Directorate's core mandate is to implement a robust quality assurance framework that aligns with the National Qualification Framework (NQF). It functions as a bridge between the university's academic activities and national quality benchmarks, ensuring that every degree program meets the highest rigorous standards.
The Directorate is committed to fostering a culture of academic integrity and continuous improvement. It actively oversees the evaluation of teaching and research through systematic feedback from students, alumni, and stakeholders. A key priority of the QEC is the capacity building of faculty and staff; it regularly organizes workshops on pedagogical skills, fair assessment techniques, and the preparation of Self-Assessment Reports (SARs) for various departments.
By prioritizing transparency and efficiency, the QEC ensures that UoBS provides a competitive learning environment. Our mission remains focused on creating a synergy between curriculum development, innovative research, and student-centered learning, ultimately transforming the university into a premier institution of global academic excellence.
Prof. Dr. Abdul Matin
Director QEC
"Quality is the continuous pursuit of excellence through academic integrity, innovative teaching, and robust evaluation, ensuring our graduates meet global standards while fostering an environment of constant improvement."
director.qec@uobs.edu.pk
Our Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Mission
The Directorate of Quality Enhancement Cell is committed to fostering a culture of academic excellence at the University of Baltistan, Skardu, by implementing robust quality assurance frameworks aligned with HEC standards. We strive to enhance teaching, research, and institutional efficiency through continuous monitoring, systematic feedback, and capacity building, ensuring our graduates and academic programs meet the highest national and international benchmarks of success.
Vision
To transform the University of Baltistan, Skardu, into a premier seat of learning recognized for global academic excellence. We envision a future where a self-sustaining culture of quality, innovation, and integrity permeates every aspect of the university, ensuring our graduates are internationally competitive and our institutional standards serve as a benchmark for higher education in the region and beyond.
Objectives
•
Implement HEC quality frameworks
•
Monitor academic program standards
•
Enhance faculty teaching skills
•
Collect systematic stakeholder feedback
•
Prepare departmental assessment reports

Key Achievement
Upon joining the University of Baltistan, Skardu, as Additional Director QEC on February 07, 2022, Dr. Attarad Ali did not receive any official QEC records or documentation despite repeated requests to the former Director QEC and relevant offices. In the absence of institutional handover, he independently initiated the establishment of the QEC in its true spirit by developing a comprehensive documentation system and operational framework. Through sustained diligence and commitment, he led the institutional transition toward PSG-2023, coordinated departments to fulfil the requirements of the respective accreditation councils, ensured the implementation of HEC policies and procedures, convened five (05) IQC meetings chaired by the VC, formulated more than 25 institutional policies through the IQC, developed institutional and academic strategic plans, designed KPIs for administrative and academic directorates and heads, and successfully conducted the first Self-RIPE of UoBS, including the Corrective Action Plan, in line with the HEC Revamped QA Framework.

•

•

Study Tour
Featured
1 min read
Department of Business Management Conducts Study Trip to Ghanche
September 12, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for Department of Business Management Conducts Study Trip to Ghanche
The Department of Business Management, Commerce & Economics, University of Baltistan, Skardu organized an educational and recreational trip to Cascade Resort Ghawari and Balghar Meadow, Ghanche.
Over 300 students and faculty members participated in the trip. At Cascade Resort, students attended a briefing session with the Resort Manager on hospitality operations, human resource management, financial management, and marketing strategies.
At Balghar Meadow, students conducted a field-based market analysis to assess local and tourist demographics and identify potential business opportunities.
The activity aimed to enhance students’ understanding of business operations and entrepreneurship in Gilgit-Baltistan. The department, on behalf of the University, extended sincere gratitude to the management and staff of Cascade Resort for their cooperation and hospitality.

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Sports Directorate
Fostering Excellence, Discipline, and a Culture of Active Living at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
Directorate of Sports
Introduction
The Sports Directorate at the University of Baltistan (UoBs) plays a vital role in promoting physical fitness, teamwork, and a spirit of healthy competition among students. It is responsible for organizing and managing a wide range of sports activities, tournaments, and interdepartmental competitions throughout the academic year.
The directorate aims to nurture talent, encourage student participation in both indoor and outdoor sports, and represent the university at regional and national levels. By providing facilities, training opportunities, and structured programs, the Sports Directorate contributes to the overall development and well-being of students at UoBs.
In 2025, for the first time, the Sports Directorate got the chance to organize Sports Gala 2025 at our new campus. Governor Gilgit-Baltistan Syed Mehdi Shah inaugurated the newly built sports complex and appreciated the Sports Directorate for organizing and managing such a beautiful event.
Furthermore, the directorate has always encouraged sports activities, whether inside the campus or outside. We enthusiastically participate in sports events organized by district administration and the Sports Board of Gilgit-Baltistan. We are hopeful that now we will participate in the Zonal and Regional sports events organized by the HEC in the future.
Junaid Ahmad
Deputy Director Sports
"Sports have the power to transform lives. At UoBS, we are committed to providing our students with the facilities, training, and opportunities they need to excel both on and off the field."
deputy.d.sports@uobs.edu.pk
500+
Active Student Athletes
15+
Sports Activities Yearly
10+
Departments Participating
100%
Student Participation Rate
Vision
To become a leading university sports directorate that fosters excellence, discipline, and a culture of active living, empowering students to achieve success in both sports and life.
Mission
To promote physical fitness, health, and well-being among students through regular sports activities.
To identify and nurture athletic talent and provide opportunities for skill development.
To organize structured competitions and events that encourage teamwork, leadership, and sportsmanship.
To represent the University of Baltistan at regional and national platforms with pride and excellence.
To provide quality sports facilities and a supportive environment for all students.
Core Focus Areas
Objectives of Sports Directorate
Guiding principles that drive our sports programs and initiatives
01
Promote active and healthy lifestyle among students
02
Encourage students to participate in co-curricular and extra-curricular activities
03
Identifying and nurturing sports talents
04
Organize inter-department sports events
05
Participate in departmental sports events organized by Sports Board and District Administration Baltistan
06
Enhance University Image through sports events

Our Strengths
Key Strengths
Foundational pillars that support our sports programs
Active participation of students in indoor and outdoor sports activities across departments.
Availability of basic sports grounds and multipurpose open spaces within the university.
Sports activities contributing positively to students' physical fitness, teamwork, leadership, and discipline.
Institutional recognition of sports as an essential component of student development and campus life.
Encouraging approach of students towards sports activities.

Facilities for indoor games and activities.
Future Outlook
With improved infrastructure, funding, and strategic planning, the directorate can significantly enhance sports culture and contribution to holistic growth at the University of Baltistan.
Plans include:
Participation in HEC Zonal and Regional sports events
Development of additional sports facilities
Introduction of new sports disciplines
Establishment of sports scholarships
Inter-university sports collaborations

Conclusion
Sports Directorate University of Baltistan always strives to provide best possible sports facilities and plays a positive role in student development and well-being during the year 2024-25. This was our first experience in the new campus, and the sports complex is already being built. With improved infrastructure, funding and strategic planning, the directorate can significantly enhance sports culture and contribution to holistic growth at the University of Baltistan.

Fee Breakdown Explanation
Understanding the fee structure components
Admission Fee
One-time fee payable at the time of admission. Covers processing and administrative costs.
Rs. 7,700
Security (Refundable)
Refundable security deposit. Returned upon completion of the program after clearance.
Rs. 4,400
Total (A)
One-time payment = Admission Fee + Security Deposit
Rs. 12,100
Semester Fees & Charges
Per semester charges including tuition, library, lab, sports, and other fees.
Varies by program
Total (B)
Per semester total fees
Same as Semester Fees
G-Total (A+B)
Total payable in first semester = One-time fees + First semester fees
Sum of Total (A) and Total (B)
Payment Information
Deadlines for fee submission
Payment Deadlines
First Semester Fee Submission
Deadline:
At the time of admission confirmation

SKARDU
Directorate of Academics
Planning, Developing, and Maintaining the Highest Standards of Academic Excellence
Directorate of Academics
Introduction
The Directorate of Academics at University of Baltistan, Skardu is the central authority responsible for planning, developing, and maintaining the highest standards of academic excellence across all faculties and departments. We ensure that academic programs are aligned with national and international standards, foster innovation in teaching and learning, and promote research-based education. Our directorate serves as the backbone of the university's academic framework, coordinating curriculum development, faculty development, quality assurance, and academic governance. We work to create an environment where academic excellence thrives, ensuring that students receive quality education that prepares them for professional success and societal contributions.
+92-05815-960058
academics@uobs.edu.pk
Dr. Ghulam Raza
Director Academics
director.academics@uobs.edu.pk
"Academic excellence is not just about imparting knowledge; it's about creating an environment where curiosity thrives, innovation flourishes, and students are empowered to become lifelong learners and contributors to society."
Mission
To develop and implement innovative academic policies, promote excellence in teaching and learning, and ensure the highest quality of education that meets national and international standards. We strive to create a dynamic learning environment that fosters intellectual growth, critical thinking, and professional development for both students and faculty members, ultimately contributing to the socio-economic development of the region and the nation.
Vision
To become a nationally recognized center of academic excellence that produces globally competent graduates, fosters innovation, and contributes to the socio-economic development of the region. We envision a university where academic programs are benchmarked against international standards, where research informs teaching, and where graduates are equipped with the knowledge, skills, and values to lead positive change in their communities and beyond.
Core Focus Areas
Objectives
Guiding principles of the Directorate of Academics
✓ Develop and implement academic policies
✓ Ensure quality assurance in programs
✓ Promote faculty development and research
✓ Enhance student learning experience
✓ Foster innovation in teaching methodologies

Regulatory Framework
Key Academic Policies
Governing framework for academic operations and quality assurance
Admission Policy
Guidelines and criteria for student admissions across all programs ensuring merit-based and transparent selection.
Examination Policy
Comprehensive framework for conduct of examinations, grading, assessment, and result declaration.
Faculty Appointment Policy
Standards and procedures for recruitment, selection, and appointment of teaching faculty.
Academic Integrity Policy
Guidelines promoting honesty, preventing plagiarism, and ensuring ethical academic practices.
Quality Assurance Framework
Mechanisms for continuous quality improvement aligned with HEC standards and accreditation requirements.
Academic Calendar Policy
Structure and schedule for semester activities, examinations, holidays, and academic events.
Governance Structure
Academic Committees
Bodies ensuring effective academic governance and quality assurance
Academic Council
Quarterly Meetings
The supreme academic body responsible for approving academic programs, policies, and regulations.
Board of Studies
Monthly Meetings
Reviews curriculum, recommends course improvements, and ensures program relevance.
Quality Assurance Committee
Continuous Process
Monitors academic quality, implements QA frameworks, and ensures HEC compliance.
Academic Regulations
Semester Rules & Academic Regulations
Comprehensive academic policies governing semester operations, assessment, and student progression
Semester Structure
Academic year consists of two regular semesters: Fall (September-December) and Spring (February-May)
Each semester spans 16-18 weeks including teaching, examinations, and breaks
Summer semester (6-8 weeks) offered for remedial/elective courses
Minimum 15 credit hours per semester for full-time students
Attendance Policy
Minimum 75% attendance required in each course for exam eligibility
Students with 65-75% attendance may appear with penalty after approval
Below 65% attendance results in course repetition
Medical emergencies require documented proof within 7 days
Assessment & Grading
Continuous assessment: 40% (Assignments, quizzes, presentations, mid-term)
Final examination: 60% of total grade
Grading Scale: A (90-100), B (80-89), C (70-79), D (60-69), F (Below 60)
CGPA required for graduation: Minimum 2.5/4.0
Examination Rules
Mid-term examinations: Week 8-9 of semester
Final examinations: Week 17-18 of semester
Unfair means during exam leads to cancellation and disciplinary action
Re-sit examinations available for failed courses (with fee)
Course Registration
Course registration within first two weeks of semester
Late registration allowed with penalty fee up to one week
Course withdrawal within first eight weeks (W grade)
Maximum credit limit: 21 credit hours per semester
Promotion & Progression
Minimum 2.0 CGPA required for promotion to next semester
Probation status for CGPA between 1.7-2.0 for one semester
Expulsion if CGPA remains below 1.7 after probation period
Maximum duration for BS degree: 7 years, MS/MPhil: 4 years

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Our Guiding Principles
Vision & Mission
Shaping the future of higher education in Gilgit-Baltistan through innovation, excellence, and community engagement
Our Vision
Our Mission
Core Values
Our Direction
Vision & Mission
Our Vision
"To become a global leader in environmental stewardship and sustainable development, harnessing the unique natural heritage of Gilgit-Baltistan, and safeguarding the ecological integrity of the Third Pole, Water Towers of South Asia, and the highest mountain ranges of the world for future generations."
Our Mission
"To advance knowledge, innovation, and community engagement in environmental conservation, sustainable resource management, and eco-friendly technologies, while promoting cultural heritage and socio-economic development in Gilgit-Baltistan, with a focus on:"
•
Protecting and preserving the region's fragile ecosystems, glaciers, rivers, and natural habitats
•
Conducting cutting-edge research and education in environmental sciences, sustainability, and climate change
•
Fostering community-based initiatives and partnerships for environmental conservation and sustainable development
•
Showcasing the region's unique biodiversity hotspots, including Deosai, Central Karakorum, and Khunjrab National Parks
•
Empowering local communities to become custodians of their natural resources and cultural heritage
Foundational Principles
Our Core Values
The principles that guide every decision, action, and interaction at University of Baltistan
Human Dignity
We respect the inherent worth and rights of every individual, fostering an environment where all members of our community are treated with respect, fairness, and compassion.
Moral Sanctity
We uphold the highest ethical standards, practicing honesty, integrity, and accountability in all academic and administrative endeavors.
Social Justice
We champion fairness, equality, and inclusion, working to eliminate barriers and create opportunities for all members of society.
Peace and Tolerance
We promote mutual understanding, respect for diversity, and peaceful coexistence among individuals of different backgrounds, cultures, and beliefs.
Eco-friendliness
We are committed to environmental sustainability, promoting practices that protect natural resources and foster ecological balance for future generations.
Academic Excellence
We pursue the highest standards of teaching, learning, and scholarship, fostering intellectual curiosity, critical thinking, and innovation.
