University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Faculty of Life Sciences
Exploring biological systems, biodiversity, and health sciences for sustainable development
Introduction
The Faculty of Life Sciences at University of Baltistan is envisioned as a center of excellence for education, research, innovation, and community engagement in the biological and health sciences. Established in the unique ecological and geographical landscape of Gilgit-Baltistan, the Faculty seeks to harness the immense potential of the region’s rich biodiversity, alpine ecosystems, indigenous livestock resources, medicinal plants, and unique microbial diversity for sustainable development and scientific advancement.
Gilgit-Baltistan is recognized as one of the world’s important ecological regions, possessing diverse flora and fauna, high-altitude rangelands, glaciers, freshwater resources, and endemic wildlife species. The region serves as a natural laboratory for scientific exploration in biodiversity conservation, wildlife ecology, climate change, mountain agriculture, livestock management, biotechnology, public health, and environmental sustainability. In response to these opportunities and regional needs, the Faculty of Life Sciences has been established with an interdisciplinary approach to produce skilled professionals, researchers, and innovators capable of addressing emerging biological, environmental, veterinary, and health-related challenges.
In its initial phase, the Faculty comprises the Departments of Zoology, Botany, Microbiology, Biotechnology, Veterinary Sciences, and Health Sciences. These departments are strategically designed to promote research and academic excellence in areas such as wildlife conservation, medicinal and alpine plants, microbial diversity, biotechnology applications, livestock and yak management, zoonotic disease surveillance, food security, and public health improvement. The Faculty aims to integrate modern scientific knowledge with indigenous wisdom and local resources to contribute effectively to the socio-economic uplift of Gilgit-Baltistan and beyond.
Through quality education, applied research, and collaboration with national and international institutions, the Faculty of Life Sciences aspires to become a leading academic and research hub in mountain biology, biodiversity conservation, veterinary sciences, and health innovation.
Our Mission
To advance knowledge in life sciences through innovative research, quality education, and community engagement for sustainable development.
Our Vision
To become a nationally and internationally recognized center of excellence in life sciences education, research, and innovation, dedicated to the sustainable utilization and conservation of the unique biodiversity, natural resources, livestock wealth, and public health systems of Gilgit-Baltistan and other mountain regions.
Key Focus Areas
Conservation Biology
Medicinal Plants
Wildlife Genetics
Microbial Diversity
Cancer Biology
Epidemiology
Climate Change Impact
Ethnobotany

SKARDU
Department of Computer Science
Innovate. Integrate. Impact. Excellence in computing education and research.
Established: 2013
NCEAC Accredited
694+ Students
Introduction
The Department of Computer Science, is one of the pioneer department of the University. The department was established in 2012 in the Karakoram International University, Skardu campus which, later reconstituted as University of Baltistan, Skardu in 2017.
Computer science is the study of the theory, experimentation, and engineering. It is an exciting, growing, challenging and one of the most demanding field in the world. The degree in Computer Science not only provides foundational knowledge of the filed, it also leads to the highest-paying, fastest-growing jobs globally.
The Department is equipped with a Computer lab with latest PCs, high-speed internet, and verity of books in the library. Our main objective is to prepare our students for their future careers in industry and research. We offer market-oriented courses aligned with HEC curriculum to achieve the objective of producing highly talented computer professionals for the job market as Software Engineers, Entrepreneur, and academia professionals.
Department Statistics
694+
Total Students

Academic Leadership
Vice Chancellor's
Message
A Message from Prof. Dr. Masood Akhtar, Vice Chancellor of University of Baltistan, Skardu
VICE CHANCELLOR
It is my privilege to welcome you to the University of Baltistan, Skardu, an emerging center of higher learning in the country. It is located in one of the world's most magnificent mountain regions, where the Himalayas meet the Karakoram and the timeless Indus River shapes its cultural and intellectual landscape. Baltistan, renowned for its natural beauty, indigenous knowledge, and culture of peace and hospitality, provides an exceptional environment for academic growth.
Spanning over an area of 70,000 sq. Km, Baltistan is home to the world's greatest mountain ranges outside the polar regions. Its valleys, peaks, glaciers, and lakes collectively create a natural university without walls. Every year, thousands of visitors, researchers, and nature enthusiasts visit this region. Amid a matchless environment, our university proudly serves as a beacon of opportunity and learning.
By integrating entrepreneurial thinking, creative problem-solving, and advanced technologies, including artificial intelligence, we are developing an academic ecosystem that bridges locally rooted knowledge with global standards of excellence. We aim to instill in our students the skills of critical thinking, intellectual independence, and creativity, addressing the socio-economic needs of our region while adhering to international standards.
We also intend to prepare graduates who can become exemplary hosts for global tourism, curators of ecological preservation, and ambassadors of our region's exceptional natural beauty. Our faculty and administrative teams collaborate to create a student-focused environment that fosters academic growth through an interactive learning experience, innovation, and exploration of new opportunities.
As we evolve, we intend to focus on making the University not only a provider of degrees but a catalyst for employability, entrepreneurship, and professional growth. We intend to strengthen our engagement with industry, government, tourism stakeholders, and local enterprises to align academic programs with emerging economic and technological needs and opportunities.
Efforts are already underway to promote innovation in sustainable tourism, renewable energy, organic agriculture, cultural heritage, and the development of eco-friendly interventions. At the same time, we recognize the global transformation brought by digital technologies and artificial intelligence; therefore, we are integrating digital literacy, ICT skills, and AI-supported learning into our academic programs and training so that our students remain competitive on both national and international platforms.
Baltistan's landscape itself is a living classroom, offering rich opportunities for inquiry in ecology, geology, environmental science, agriculture, tourism, and cultural studies. Our vision is to transform the University into a center of knowledge to address local challenges through actionable solutions that benefit our communities and contribute to broader scientific understanding.
I invite all students, faculty, staff, alumni, and partners to join us in this exciting journey of building a university that not only provides quality education but also serves as a catalyst for positive change in our region and beyond.
Together, let us harness the unique potential of our location and heritage to create an institution that stands as a testament to the power of education in transforming lives and communities.
With warm regards and commitment to excellence,
Vice Chancellor
University of Baltistan, Skardu
Leadership Team
January 2025

Meeting
4 min read
UoBS and GIZ Pakistan Discuss Strengthening Career Services, Job Placement and Entrepreneurship Opportunities
September 3, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for UoBS and GIZ Pakistan Discuss Strengthening Career Services, Job Placement and Entrepreneurship Opportunities
Islamabad, September 03, 2026: The Vice Chancellor of the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS), held a meeting with Ms. Sabrina Gable, GIZ Pakistan, in Islamabad on September 03, 2026, to discuss ongoing collaboration under the EU Mission through the TVET SSP Project and initiatives aimed at strengthening career development, employment opportunities, entrepreneurship, and public-private sector collaboration in Gilgit-Baltistan.
The meeting was attended by Mr. Abdullah bin Kamal, Career & Entrepreneurship Advisor, GIZ, Dr. Ibrahim Hussain, Focal Person for the EU Mission under the TVET SSP Project, and Mr. Zeeshan Ali, Regional Director, NAVTTC Gilgit-Baltistan.
During the meeting, the participants discussed preparations for the Job Fair at UoBS, expected to be held on September 22, 2026. The participants emphasised the importance of effective coordination among relevant stakeholders to ensure the successful organisation of the job fair and to provide meaningful employment and career opportunities to university graduates and students.
The meeting also discussed the inauguration of the Career Counselling and Job Placement Office (CCJPO) at UoBS, which has been established with the support of GIZ. The CCJPO is already established and functional at the University, and the CCJP Officer has also been formally notified. The office is expected to play an important role in connecting students and graduates with employers, providing career counselling, facilitating job placement, and strengthening linkages between academia and industry.
Another key area of discussion was support for the Business Incubation Center (BIC) at UoBS. The initiative aims to promote entrepreneurship, innovation, and enterprise development among students and graduates and contribute to the development of a skilled and entrepreneurial workforce in Gilgit-Baltistan.
The Vice Chancellor assured the GIZ representatives that all requirements necessary for the timely implementation of the project activities would be fulfilled at the earliest. He reaffirmed UoBS's commitment to close coordination with GIZ and other stakeholders to ensure that the activities under the project are implemented smoothly and successfully.
The Vice Chancellor was also briefed on the outcomes of the 2nd Workshop on Enhancing Public-Private Partnership Collaboration in Pakistan's Technical Education System, supported by GIZ and conducted by the Collective Leadership Institute (CLI), Germany. The workshop brought together members of the Action Team Gilgit-Baltistan, including representatives from UoBS, Karakoram International University (KIU), Gilgit-Baltistan Chamber of Commerce and Industry (GBCCI), Women Chamber of Commerce, NAVTTC, and GBTEST.
As an outcome of the workshop, the Action Team Gilgit-Baltistan proposed the development of competency-based curricula for two trades, Administrative Assistant and Finance Assistant, through NAVTTC. The proposed initiative is aimed at strengthening the human resource capacity of local Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) in Gilgit-Baltistan. NAVTTC has approved the proposal, and the curriculum development process is expected to commence soon. The programs are intended to be offered particularly through the two universities of Gilgit-Baltistan, UoBS and KIU, thereby providing local youth with industry-relevant skills and improving their employability.
The Action Team Gilgit-Baltistan also proposed the establishment of a regional platform titled “Gilgit-Baltistan Public Private Partnership and Regional Innovation (GBPPPRI)”. A draft Terms of Reference (ToRs) has already been developed with the objective of establishing a sustainable mechanism for public-private partnership and regional innovation in Gilgit-Baltistan.
The Vice Chancellor appreciated the efforts of the Action Team Gilgit-Baltistan and expressed his full support for the proposed GBPPPRI Council. He further announced that UoBS would allocate office space for the Council to facilitate its operations and coordination and to support its efforts toward strengthening sustainable public-private partnerships and regional innovation in Gilgit-Baltistan.
The meeting concluded with a shared commitment to strengthening collaboration among UoBS, GIZ, NAVTTC, industry, chambers of commerce, and other public and private sector stakeholders to enhance employability, entrepreneurship, skills development, and sustainable economic opportunities for the people of Gilgit-Baltistan.

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Fully Funded Master's Scholarship (China)
Mr. Shakir Hussain
BS Botany, 2018-2022 (Inaugural Cohort)
Botany
China
Research
Success Story
Syed Shakir Hussain is a distinguished graduate from the inaugural cohort of the University of Baltistan in Skardu, making history as the first student from both his university and the Baltistan region to be accepted into a Master's program in China. Currently studying at the prestigious Chinese Academy of Agricultural Sciences (CAAS), China's leading agricultural research institute, he is advancing his education in the fields of agriculture, the environment, and energy.
Syed Shakir Hussain earned his Bachelor of Science in Botany at the University of Baltistan as part of its pioneering batch. Throughout his studies, he demonstrated exceptional academic performance, critical thinking abilities, and a growing passion for agriculture, environmental preservation, and biology. As one of the university's first graduates, he played a key role in shaping the early academic programs of his department. His burgeoning interest in research laid a strong foundation for his international academic journey.
Syed Shakir's academic excellence and research potential culminated in a rare and remarkable achievement: a fully funded Master's scholarship from CAAS, a first for both the University of Baltistan and the Baltistan region. After careful consideration, he accepted the offer, furthering his education at one of China's most prestigious research institutions.
At CAAS, Syed is focusing on waste management for biogas production, an essential area in renewable energy and environmental conservation. His research is dedicated to developing sustainable and effective methods to convert waste into energy, a crucial endeavor for regions like Gilgit-Baltistan, which face unique environmental and resource challenges.
Syed Shakir Hussain's success story highlights the growing strength of the University of Baltistan and its ability to prepare students for international achievement. His journey serves as an inspiration to students from remote and underserved areas, demonstrating that with perseverance, vision, and dedication, even those from the most challenging backgrounds can reach remarkable heights on the global stage.
About This Achievement
Mr. Shakir Hussain achieved
fully funded master's scholarship (china) while studying
BS Botany, 2018-2022 (Inaugural Cohort).
This remarkable accomplishment demonstrates the quality of education and opportunities available at University of Baltistan.

Treasurer Office
Financial stewardship and budgetary excellence for sustainable academic growth
About Treasurer Office
The Treasurer Office of the University of Baltistan plays a central role in ensuring the financial integrity, transparency, and sustainability of the University. It is responsible for the effective management of financial resources in accordance with government regulations, university statutes, and approved financial policies.
The office oversees the preparation and implementation of the annual budget, ensuring optimal allocation of funds to academic, administrative, and developmental activities. It maintains accurate financial records, manages receipts and payments, and ensures proper utilization of public funds. The Treasurer Office is also responsible for processing salaries, pensions, scholarships, and other payments in a timely and efficient manner.
Among its key functions are financial planning, budget forecasting, monitoring expenditures, and preparing financial statements and reports for internal and external stakeholders. The office facilitates internal and external audits and ensures compliance with applicable financial rules and standards.
Additionally, the Treasurer Office manages grants, development funds, and special project accounts, while providing financial guidance to various departments of the University. It works in close coordination with university management to support strategic initiatives and institutional growth.
Committed to transparency, accountability, and service excellence, the Treasurer Office strives to strengthen financial governance and contribute to the overall mission and vision of the University of Baltistan.
Digital Transformation
The working of the department has been converted into an electronic data processing system using in-house developed software. This digital transformation has greatly contributed to achieving organizational management efficiency and meeting complex accounting needs of the growing university.
Mr. Muhammad Askari
Treasurer

Information Technology Directorate
Driving digital transformation and technological innovation at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
Digital Innovation
Introduction
Established in 2012, the Directorate of Information Technology at the University of Baltistan (UoBS) has been the cornerstone of the institution's digital transformation, distinguished by its development of a comprehensive, in-house Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) system tailored to the university's unique needs.
The Directorate's critical role was particularly evident during the pandemic, when it enabled a seamless asynchronous learning environment that ensured uninterrupted education for both faculty and students. This dedication to technological advancement culminated in UoBS being recognized as a Smart University under the HEC's Smart University Project, a testament to its success in fostering a modern digital learning ecosystem that elevates educational experiences and operational efficiency.
Today, the directorate continues to drive this mission by meticulously managing the university's entire network infrastructure and leading all in-house software development initiatives.
Mr. Amjad Ali
IT Incharge
"Since 2012, our Directorate has built UoBS's in-house ERP and managed its network infrastructure. Around the end of 2024, we established a new switch-to-switch fiber optic connection across the university. Now recognized as a Smart University, we are excited to integrate AI into our systems in near future to further empower our students and faculty."
amjad.ali@uobs.edu.pk
Our Strategic Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Our Mission
To empower the University of Baltistan by developing and maintaining innovative in-house software solutions, including our comprehensive ERP, and managing a robust, future-ready network infrastructure. We are committed to enhancing educational experiences and operational efficiency by integrating emerging technologies like Artificial Intelligence, ensuring UoBS remains a leader in digital learning.
Our Vision
To be the driving force behind a fully intelligent and seamlessly connected University of Baltistan, where innovative in-house solutions and a state-of-the-art fiber optic backbone empower a transformative educational experience.

Milestones & Impact
Key Achievements
Significant contributions and technological milestones achieved under the leadership of Mr. Imtiaz Ahmed
📊
ERP Enhancements – QEC Module
Successfully developed and integrated the Quality Enhancement Cell (QEC) module into the ERP, enabling comprehensive student and teacher evaluations along with automated evaluation reports.
✓
ERP Enhancements – Accreditation Module
Designed and implemented an Accreditation module facilitating digital management of course folders and ensuring full compliance with NCEAC accreditation requirements.
📋
ERP Enhancements – Student Reports
Introduced various customizable student report formats to meet the evolving data requirements of HEC and other regulatory bodies.
💰
ERP Enhancements – Accounts Module
Upgraded and optimized the Accounts module in close coordination with the Treasurer's office to align with updated financial procedures and requirements.
🏫
Smart Classroom Establishment
Established a state-of-the-art Smart Classroom in collaboration with Sukkur IBA and LUMS, funded by the European Commission, to promote technology-enhanced learning.
💻
Data Center Establishment
Designed and commissioned a modern, state-of-the-art Data Center to centrally host and manage the ERP system and network services for students, faculty, and staff.
🌐
New Campus Network Infrastructure
Planned and executed the complete network setup for the UoBS new campus, ensuring seamless digital connectivity from day one.
🔌
Fiber Optic Connectivity
Successfully extended high-speed fiber optic connectivity to the new campus, integrating it with the university's main switch-to-switch backbone established in late 2024.

A Legacy of Spiritual Excellence
Foundation & Construction
The University Mosque was established in
2026
with the vision of creating
a spiritual center that complements the academic excellence of our institution.
Year Established:
2026
Total Area:
8,500 sq. ft.
Capacity:
500 worshippers
Architectural Significance

atmosphere for worship and reflection.
Modern Islamic architectural style
Elegant calligraphy and geometric patterns
State-of-the-art sound system
Separate prayer areas for men and women

2 min read
HEC Monitoring Team Visits Technology Incubation Center Kharmang
September 8, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for HEC Monitoring Team Visits Technology Incubation Center Kharmang
September 08, 2026: A monitoring team from the Higher Education Commission (HEC) of Pakistan visited the Technology Incubation Center (TIC) Kharmang to review its activities and progress. Director ORIC, Dr. Munawwar Ali Abbas, warmly welcomed the HEC monitoring team and briefed them about the initiatives and activities being carried out through the Technology Incubation Centers established by the University of Baltistan, Skardu, in Shigar, Ghanche, Kharmang, and Skardu. He highlighted the University's efforts to provide skills development, entrepreneurship opportunities, and capacity-building programs for the youth of the region. The HEC team appreciated the activities and efforts of the University of Baltistan, Skardu, and assured their full cooperation and support in the future to further strengthen and expand the TICs. During the briefing, the Director ORIC also conveyed the vision of the Vice Chancellor, Prof. Dr. Masood Akhter, regarding the main objectives of the Technology Incubation Centers, particularly the engagement and empowerment of local youth through diverse skills and livelihood opportunities, including livestock management, agriculture, entrepreneurship, freelancing, digital marketing, handicrafts, and other market-oriented skills. At the end of the visit, HEC Director Monitoring Dr. Bashir Khan and Deputy Director Monitoring Mr. Yasir Anwar distributed certificates among the participants who successfully completed the training programs.

Student Societies & Clubs
Where passion meets purpose - Explore diverse student-led organizations
Student Societies at UoBS
The University of Baltistan hosts a vibrant array of student societies that provide
platforms for academic exploration, professional development, cultural exchange,
and personal growth. These student-led organizations enrich campus life and create
opportunities for leadership and community engagement.
Each society operates under faculty mentorship while being primarily run by students,
offering hands-on experience in event management, teamwork, and organizational leadership.
Featured Societies
Discover your passion through our diverse range of student organizations
UoBS Computing Society

technological innovation, coding skills, and professional development in
the digital age.

Meeting
Featured
1 min read
Consultative meeting on the design and development of the underconstruction Balti Museum at University
September 9, 2026
University of Baltistan
Featured image for Consultative meeting on the design and development of the underconstruction Balti Museum at University
The Directorate of Planning & Development, University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS), held a consultative meeting on the design and development of the underconstruction Balti Museum at University.
November 09, 2026
The meeting was attended by Dr. Zara, CEO Lajward and former Head of Architecture, NCA; Nazir Ahmad, CEO Baltistan Cultural and Development Foundation; Rahat Ali, Regional Programme Manager, AKRSP; landscape designer Anwar Ali;Project Director and Director P&D Dr.Zakir Hussain and his team presented the proposed museum layout. The experts shared valuable suggestions to ensure that the design authentically reflects the cultural heritage and indigenous identity of Baltistan.
The recommendations will be incorporated into the ongoing design process to further strengthen the museum’s cultural and architectural character.

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
VC Secretariat
Strategic leadership support and executive coordination at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
About VC Secretariat
The Vice Chancellor’s Secretariat serves as the central executive office of the University of Baltistan, Skardu. It provides strategic, administrative, and coordination support to the Vice Chancellor in the execution of academic, administrative, financial, and policy matters of the University. The Secretariat ensures smooth communication between the Vice Chancellor and all statutory bodies, academic departments, administrative offices, external stakeholders, and regulatory authorities. It plays a pivotal role in policy implementation, institutional planning, and monitoring of university activities. The office upholds professionalism, confidentiality, efficiency, and transparency while facilitating decision-making processes. Through effective coordination and timely execution of tasks, the Secretariat contributes significantly to achieving the University's vision of academic excellence and institutional development.
Our Mission
The mission of the Vice Chancellor’s Secretariat is to provide efficient, transparent, and strategic support to the Vice Chancellor in implementing the University’s policies and vision. It coordinates academic, research, and operational activities across all departments and directorates, facilitates collaboration with national and international partners and regulatory bodies, and ensures timely execution of decisions, effective communication, and proper documentation to uphold governance, accountability, and institutional excellence.
Key Functions & Responsibilities
Comprehensive executive support and administrative coordination
Role in Governance
Functions as the nerve center of university governance
Ensures VC vision and policies are effectively communicated and implemented
Daily Briefings
Brief the Vice-Chancellor about daily engagements
Ensure preparedness for scheduled events and urgent matters
Mail Scrutiny
Receive, examine, prioritize, and process all correspondence
Obtain necessary orders from VC for action
Meeting Arrangements
Organize meetings with delegations and stakeholders
Schedule VC-presided sessions with faculty and bodies
Action Monitoring
Track departmental actions and follow up on decisions
Report compliance status and ensure timely execution
Liaison Coordination
Maintain communication with Deans, Directors, HoDs
Coordinate with HEC and external authorities
Executive Assistance
Support policy matters, reports, and documentation
Provide administrative and academic support to VC

Guiding principles that define our professional conduct

Powers and Duties of the Syndicate
The Syndicate is the executive body of the University and shall exercise general supervision over the affairs and management of the University in accordance with the UoBS Order, 2016.
Executive body responsible for implementing policies and decisions
Supervise academic, administrative, and financial matters
Manage University property, funds, and investments
Approve annual budget and financial statements
Manage University infrastructure and facilities
Appoint administrative staff and officers (excluding academic appointments)
Enter into contracts on behalf of the University
Ensure compliance with statutes and regulations
Monitor implementation of Senate decisions
Oversee planning and development activities
Ensure proper maintenance of University assets
Execute academic and administrative policies

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
The Region
About Baltistan
The Majestic Land of Giants - Where Nature, Culture, and History Converge
Geography
Culture
Land of Majestic Mountains
Baltistan is a region of exceptional natural beauty, cultural richness, and strategic significance, located in the northernmost part of Pakistan. Renowned for its towering peaks, pristine valleys, and historic heritage, Baltistan is home to some of the world’s highest mountains, including K2. The region is distinguished by its resilient and hospitable people, deep-rooted traditions, and a strong sense of community. Baltistan’s unique geographic position and cultural diversity make it not only a place of scenic wonder but also a center of emerging educational, economic, and social development.
With its breathtaking landscapes and peaceful environment, Baltistan offers an ideal setting for learning, research, and innovation. The region is increasingly recognized for its potential in tourism, climate studies, sustainable development, and mountain research. As an area rich in history and opportunity, Baltistan continues to inspire progress while preserving its heritage, making it a vital part of Pakistan’s national identity and future growth.
Home to K2 (8,611m)
Largest glaciers outside polar regions
Source of Indus River
Natural Wonders
Geographic Significance
A region defined by its dramatic landscapes and ecological importance
8,611m
K2 Peak Height
500+
Glaciers
4
8,000m Peaks
70%
Mountain Coverage
Mountain Ranges
Home to the Karakoram Range with four of the world's fourteen 8,000-meter peaks, including K2, Gasherbrum I & II, and Broad Peak.
Water Resources
Source of the Indus River, with massive glaciers including Siachen (world's longest outside polar regions) and Baltoro.
Biodiversity
Unique high-altitude ecosystems supporting snow leopards, Marco Polo sheep, Himalayan brown bears, and endemic plant species.

Cultural Heritage
Cultural Tapestry
A rich blend of Tibetan, Central Asian, and South Asian influences
Balti Language
Tibeto-Burman language with Persian script

Ancient forts and mosques

Traditional Balti dishes
Cultural Preservation
The University of Baltistan plays a crucial role in preserving and promoting Balti culture through:
Balti Language Research Center
Cultural Heritage Documentation
Traditional Arts Programs
Oral History Preservation
University of Baltistan's Regional Role
Addressing regional challenges through education and research
Climate Change Research
Studying glacier melt, water resource management, and climate adaptation strategies for mountain communities.
High-Altitude Agriculture
Research on sustainable farming practices and food security in high-altitude regions.
Sustainable Tourism
Developing responsible tourism models that benefit local communities while preserving natural resources.

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Directorate of Professional & Skill Development
Enhancing professional capabilities through policy reforms, comprehensive training programs, workshops, and certifications
About the Directorate
Introduction
The Directorate of Professional & Skill Development is dedicated to enhance professional capabilities of faculty, staff, and the wider community through policy reforms, comprehensive training programs, workshops, and certifications. The directorate was initially established as Professional Development Center in 2018. Later on, it was renamed as Directorate of Skill and Professional Development. In November 2025, the Directorate of Professional Development was constituted as a full-fledged directorate to cater to the needs of professional development within the university and the society at large. However, in March 2026, the directorate of Professional Development and Directorate of Professional Development were once again merged together as the "Directorate of Professional & Skill Development".
We bridge the gap between theory and practice with the aim to equip the stakeholders with relevant knowledge, skills, and expertise for the contemporary society and equip the youth with relevant skills as per the needs of the 21st Century. The Directorate serves as a center for lifelong learning, offering both short-term skill development courses and comprehensive certification programs. We focus on practical, hands-on training that enables participants to apply their learning immediately in professional settings.
Through industry collaborations and expert-led training, we aim to create a skilled workforce that contributes to regional economic development and meets the evolving demands of the job market.
Dr. Haji Karim Khan
Director Professional & Skill Development
University of Baltistan, Skardu is a federally chartered university functioning under the "University of Baltistan Order 2016". Since its inception, the university has been playing a key role in the core areas of teaching, research and services. Nestled in the mountains of Himalaya and Karakoram, the geographical location of the university provides a strong foundation for the current and future programmatic and research activities so as to have a long-lasting impact on the lives of the remote mountain communities. Therefore, all the activities in the Directorate of Professional & Skill Development focus on socio-economic development of the region through the provision of relevant professional learning opportunities.
Establishment of the Directorate of Professional & Skill Development is one of the key initiatives of the university to serve the internal and external stakeholders in Gilgit-Baltistan and elsewhere in the country. Thus, provision of contextually relevant professional learning opportunities to the communities within the university and the society is the key mandate of the directorate so as to provide the individuals with wider earning opportunities and social mobility. This will have a positive and transformational impact on the lives of the people.
We believe in learning together through scaffolding, sharing of ideas, and work as a team with the aim to bring about positive changes. We value contextually relevant ideas, innovative practices, and longlisting initiatives. Our faculty, students, staff and youth are our assets and together we will make a difference, InshaAllah!
director.psd@uobs.edu.pk
Our Strategic Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Our Mission
We provide contextually relevant and value-laden professional and skill development opportunities to ensure professionalism, foster industry-academia collaboration, and promote lifelong learning to our university employees and collaborators.
Our Vision
Becoming center of excellence for professional learning and human capacity building through ensuring exemplary professional ethics, innovative practices, and contextually-relevant reform initiatives.
Our Values
Human dignity
Professionalism
Inclusion
Respect
Learning
Key Objectives
•
Leading and initiating policy reforms (internal and external) concerning professional and skill development
•
Ensuring collaboration with governmental and non-governmental sectors in policy making and capacity building of professionals
•
Ensuring access to contextually relevant professional development and skills development opportunities in various fields
•
Developing and building capacity of Master Trainers in the areas of skill development
•
Arranging workshops, seminars, talks, and colloquia for the in-house professional development of various categories of stakeholders

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Japanese Government (MEXT) Scholarship
Ms. Konain Zahra Maqbool
BS Botany, Fall 2019–2023
Biology
Success Story
Konain Zahra Maqbool's academic journey is a powerful story of determination, resilience, and breaking barriers. Hailing from the Baltistan region where access to international academic opportunities is limited, she dared to dream beyond boundaries and worked persistently to turn that dream into reality.
As a graduate of the Department of Botany, University of Baltistan, Konain consistently demonstrated academic excellence, curiosity for scientific research, and a deep commitment to Botany. Her passion for sustainable plant production and scientific innovation motivated her to pursue advanced studies at the global level.
Her selection for the Japanese Government (MEXT) Scholarship, one of the world's most competitive and prestigious fully funded scholarships, marks a historic achievement. She is the first female from Baltistan and the first alumna of the Department of Botany to earn this honor, setting a precedent for future generations of students, especially young women from remote and underserved regions.
Currently based in Japan, Konain is actively engaged in Master's research in Plant Production Sciences, where she is gaining advanced knowledge, international research exposure, and cross-cultural academic experience. Her work not only contributes to scientific advancement but also holds the potential to positively impact agricultural development back home.
Konain Zahra Maqbool stands as a beacon of inspiration proving that with dedication, mentorship, and institutional support, students from Gilgit-Baltistan can compete and excel on the global academic stage. Her success reflects the growing academic strength of the University of Baltistan and reinforces its mission to nurture globally competent scholars.
About This Achievement
Ms. Konain Zahra Maqbool achieved
japanese government (mext) scholarship while studying
BS Botany, Fall 2019–2023.
This remarkable accomplishment demonstrates the quality of education and opportunities available at University of Baltistan.
Academic Program
Program Completed
BS Botany, Fall 2019–2023

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
Directorate of Advanced Studies
Fostering research excellence and postgraduate education at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
About Us
Introduction
The Directorate of Advanced Studies and Research (DASR) at the University of Baltistan, Skardu, serves as a central body for promoting and regulating postgraduate education and research activities.
It plays a pivotal role in ensuring academic excellence by overseeing MS and PhD programs, facilitating research approvals, and maintaining quality standards in line with Higher Education Commission (HEC) guidelines. DASR is responsible for processing synopses, monitoring thesis progress, organizing evaluation procedures, and ensuring transparency in research practices. The directorate also promotes a research culture by encouraging innovation, interdisciplinary collaboration, and ethical research conduct among students and faculty members.
Through effective coordination with academic departments, DASR ensures that all research activities contribute meaningfully to scientific advancement and societal development. It also maintains proper documentation, records, and communication channels to streamline academic processes. Overall, DASR acts as a backbone for research governance, aiming to enhance the university’s academic reputation and produce competent researchers capable of addressing local and global challenges..
Dr. Shafqat Hussain
Director
"Quality research thrives on integrity, innovation, and collaboration; our goal is to support scholars in generating impactful knowledge that addresses real-world challenges and advances academic excellence."
dasr@uobs.edu.pk
Our Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Mission
The mission of DASR is to promote high-quality research and advanced studies by providing a structured, transparent, and supportive academic environment. It aims to facilitate scholars and faculty through efficient research management, adherence to ethical standards, and alignment with national and international guidelines. DASR is committed to fostering innovation, critical thinking, and interdisciplinary collaboration to produce knowledge that contributes to sustainable development and societal progress.
Vision
The vision of DASR is to become a leading center of excellence in research and postgraduate studies, recognized nationally and internationally for its quality, innovation, and impact. It aspires to cultivate a vibrant research culture that empowers scholars to address emerging challenges through scientific inquiry and collaboration. By maintaining high academic standards, DASR envisions contributing to the intellectual and socio-economic development of the region and beyond.
Objectives
•
Promote quality research culture
•
Ensure ethical research practices
•
Strengthen postgraduate programs
•
Facilitate thesis evaluation processes
•
Enhance interdisciplinary collaboration

Dedicated professionals committed to supporting postgraduate education and research

Semester Rules & Regulations
University of Baltistan, Skardu - Academic Guidelines
Effective: Oct 17, 2020

Rules Development Committee

Dr. Ghulam Raza

Other Regulations
1-3. Short Title, Definitions & Scheme of Studies
1. SHORT TITLE, COMMENCEMENT AND APPLICATION
1.1
These Rules and Regulations shall be known as "The University of Baltistan Semester Rules & Regulations 2019".
1.2
These Rules shall come into force with immediate effect and shall apply in the University of Baltistan, Skardu and its constituent colleges/affiliated institutions.
2. DEFINITIONS
Academic Program
A "Program of Studies" which leads to the award of a university degree.
Credit Hour (Cr. Hr)
Teaching/learning a theory course for one hour each week throughout the semester.
CGPA
Cumulative Grade Point Average - Summation of grade points of all credit courses divided by total credit hours.
Semester
An academic period (16-18 weeks) in which one set of courses is offered.
5. ADMISSIONS
Admission Procedure
a
Admissions for BS/Bachelor (4 years) degree programs shall be made once a year.
b
Admissions shall be announced after declaration of HSSC results and completed 05 working days prior to commencement of semester.
c
Seat Distribution: 80% from Gilgit-Baltistan, 20% from across Pakistan (including foreign students).
Required Documents
Attested copies of SSC, HSSC certificates
Attested copies of detailed transcripts
Character certificate from last institution
Domicile certificate
08 passport size photographs
Migration certificate (within 30 days after admission)
CNIC or Form 'B'
Affidavit signed by applicant and father/guardian (Annex-B)
Merit Calculation Formula
Components
BS (16 years)
MA/M.Sc (16 years)
MS/M.Phil (18 years)
SSC
10%
05%
10%
HSSC
20%
10%
BA/B.Sc (14 year)
N/A
15%
BS/MA (16 year)
N/A
15%
Subject Relevancy
20 marks
N/A
Entry Test
50 marks (optional)
50 marks (mandatory)
6-7. Credit Hours & Course Layout
Credit Hours System
Theory Course
1 Credit Hour = 1 hour per week for 16 weeks
Lab/Practical
1 Credit Hour = 3 hours per week for 16 weeks
BS Program Requirement
124-140 Credit Hours total
Course Structure Examples
3(3-0)
3 credit hours theory only
4(3-1)
4 credit hours (3 theory + 1 lab)
4(1-3)
4 credit hours (1 theory + 3 lab)
13. ATTENDANCE POLICY
Minimum Requirement
75% attendance mandatory in each course
Medical Leave
Maximum 2 weeks medical leave allowed (case by case basis)
Sports/Events
10% attendance benefit for university/province/country representation
Consequences
Below 75% = Ineligible for final examination
Attendance Relaxation
10%
Chairperson can waive off 10% shortage with Rs. 1000 fee per course
30 Days
Maximum exceptional leave in one semester (must maintain 75%)
Absence Rules
2 Weeks
Absent without reason = Course admission cancelled
4 Weeks
Consecutive absence = Course dropped (must repeat)
14. EXAMINATIONS
Assessment Weightage
Courses without Practical
20%
Quizzes, Assignments, Presentations
30%
Mid Term Examination
50%
Final Term Examination
Courses with Practical
15%
Quizzes, Assignments
25%
Mid Term Examination
20%
Practical Examinations
40%
Final Term Examination
Examination Rules
Minimum 50% marks required in aggregate to pass a course
Mid-Term: Minimum 60 minutes, Final-Term: Minimum 90 minutes
No Supplementary/Special Exams - Failed courses must be repeated
Defaulters of university dues not permitted in examinations
18. THESIS/DISSERTATION PROCEDURES
MPhil Thesis Timeline
Semester 3
Thesis proposal submission within 30 days of commencement
Progress Reports
End of 3rd & 4th semesters - Two unsatisfactory reports lead to termination
Normal Duration
4 semesters (2 years) - Extendable to max 6 semesters
Submission
5 copies required before final examination
Publication Requirement
Mandatory Publication
One research paper in HEC recognized journal required before thesis submission
BS Thesis
8-12
Thesis not accepted earlier than 8th or later than 12th semester
1
Only one chance of resubmission allowed
Examination Board
Chairman/HoD (Ex-Officio)
External Examiner (One)
Research Supervisor/s (Internal)
Key Rules Summary
Academic Calendar
16-18 weeks per semester including examinations
Course Load
BS: 15-18 Cr. Hrs/semester, MS: 9-12 Cr. Hrs/semester
Course Repeat
Courses below grade C can be repeated for improvement
Program Duration
BS: Max 6 years, MS: Max 4 years (with extensions)
Disciplinary Actions
Fines from Rs. 500 to Rs. 3000 for unfair means
Freezing Semester
Allowed for valid reasons with 25% tuition fee payment

Directorate of Planning & Development
Strategic Planning, Infrastructure Development, and Institutional Growth
About P&D
Introduction
The Planning & Development Directorate at the University of Baltistan, Skardu serves as the central hub for strategic planning, infrastructure development, and institutional growth. Established to ensure systematic and sustainable development of the university, the directorate works in close coordination with all academic and administrative units to transform institutional vision into actionable development plans.
Responsible for preparing comprehensive master plans, securing funding for development projects, and overseeing their implementation, the P&D Directorate ensures that all initiatives align with academic goals, adhere to quality standards, and contribute to creating an enabling environment for teaching, learning, and research.
Dr. Zakir Hussain Zakir
Director Planning & Development
"The directorate aspire to build and transform UoBS into a model campus with sustainable infrastructure matching with local environment that fosters academic excellence and provides an inspiring environment for learning."
director.pnd@uobs.edu.pk
Our Direction
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Mission
To plan, develop, and maintain geographically resilient and eco-friendly infrastructure and resources that support academic excellence, research innovation, and sustainable growth of the University of Baltistan. We are committed to preparing comprehensive master plans, securing funding for development projects, and ensuring their successful implementation. Through strategic resource mobilization, quality assurance, and sustainable practices, we aim to create modern facilities that meet international standards while preserving our unique cultural and natural heritage.
Vision
A model sustainable university campus with state-of-the-art facilities, green environment and practices that foster academic excellence, contextually relevant research practices and learning opportunities for students and faculty. We envision a fully developed, eco-friendly campus that integrates modern infrastructure with the natural beauty of Baltistan, creating a conducive atmosphere for innovative teaching, cutting-edge research, and holistic student development, recognized nationally and internationally for its architectural excellence and sustainable practices.
Objectives
Develop comprehensive short-term and long-term master plans for campus development and extensions
Secure funding for infrastructure and development projects from HEC, PSDP, and international donors
Monitor and ensure timely completion of all development projects within approved budgets
Implement sustainable and eco-friendly practices in all construction and development initiatives
Ensure optimal utilization of university land, buildings, and other resources
Promote sustainable practices and academic/research innovations
P&D Directorate Staff
Professional team managing planning, development, and infrastructure projects

Under construction in completion phase with space and provision of 50,000+ books, digital resources, and 200 seating capacity with modern reading areas
Laboratories
10+ research and teaching labs with modern equipment for science, computer science, and engineering
Student Hostels
Girls hostel with 400 capacity, dining facilities, and recreation areas
Faculty Housing
Residential units for faculty and staff within campus
Bachelors Hostel
For residential facilities of bachelor faculty and staff
Sports Complex
Sports facilities including Hockey ground, football ground, basketball, badminton, volleyball, and gymnasium
Administrative Block
Centralized administrative offices for smooth institutional operations
Balti Museum
A unique building to exhibit the rich cultural and archaeological heritage of Baltistan
Multi-Purpose Hall
A multipurpose hall to host major events, festivities, celebrations cocurricular activities
Cafeteria and Common Areas
Modern dining and student gathering spaces
Transportation
University fleet of 10 buses for student and staff commuting
Utility Infrastructure
Water supply, electricity backup, and waste management systems. The Admin and Academic Block-01 have been fully solarized.

Expected Completion: June 2026

Directorate of Students Affairs
Fostering a disciplined, respectful, and academically supportive campus environment
About the Directorate
Introduction
The Directorate of Student Affairs at the University of Baltistan, Skardu is committed to fostering a disciplined, respectful, and academically supportive campus environment in line with the University's statutory framework. It oversees the implementation of the University's Code of Conduct, ensuring adherence to institutional policies on discipline, ethical behavior, professional decorum, and civic responsibility, thereby promoting integrity, accountability, and institutional harmony.
The Directorate also provides comprehensive support to students in planning and executing inter, intra, and extracurricular activities, including academic competitions, cultural and recreational programs, seminars, workshops, national day celebrations, sports events, galas and educational visits. It offers guidance on financial, administrative, and logistical requirements to ensure the successful organization of these activities.
In addition, the Directorate addresses student concerns through counseling, appropriate interventions, and, where necessary, parental engagement, fostering a safe, inclusive, and vibrant campus environment. By combining regulatory oversight with proactive support, the Directorate enhances student development, leadership, and holistic engagement, contributing to a dynamic and thriving university community.
Mr. Naseem Hasrat
Deputy Director, Directorate of Student Affairs
University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS)
It is my immense pleasure to welcome you to the University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS) on behalf of the Directorate of Student Affairs. The Office of Student Affairs serves as a centralized support hub, providing comprehensive assistance to students by connecting them with relevant university departments and support services. We facilitate academic guidance, career development, mental health and personal counseling, and address general inquiries, ensuring that every student receives timely and effective support to thrive academically, socially, and personally.
Your growth, success, and aspirations are our priority.
+92-5815-960061
deputy.dsa@uobs.edu.pk
Our Strategic Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Our Mission
The mission of the Directorate of Student Affairs (DSA) is to promote the holistic growth and well-being of every student through a comprehensive approach that fosters resilience, encourages engagement, ensures welfare, and instills ethical values. DSA initiatives equip students with the skills, ethical understanding, and social responsibility required to thrive academically, professionally, and socially.
Our Vision
The Directorate of Student Affairs (DSA) aspires to be a center of excellence in student development, cultivating holistic growth, ethical conduct, resilience, and environmental stewardship. Through innovative and inclusive programs, the DSA empowers students with the knowledge, skills, and values necessary to succeed academically, contribute meaningfully to society, and embrace responsible citizenship.
Our Values
Integrity
Inclusiveness
Resilience
Social Responsibility
Environmental Stewardship
Student Financial Aid
Key Objectives
•
Foster the holistic development, well-being, and resilience of every student
•
Promote ethical values, integrity, and responsible citizenship
•
Encourage active participation in co-curricular, cultural, and social initiatives
•
Provide comprehensive guidance and support for academic, personal, and career growth
•
Ensure an inclusive, diverse, and safe campus environment
•

Scholarships & Financial Aid
Empowering students through financial support and merit recognition
Academic Year: 2025-2026
500+
Students Supported Annually
PKR 50M+
Total Scholarship Amount
3
Active Programs
100%
Merit-Based Coverage
Overview
University of Baltistan, Skardu offers various scholarship programs to support deserving and talented students. These scholarships are designed to ensure that financial constraints do not hinder academic excellence.
Active Scholarship Programs
Currently open scholarship opportunities
PEF Scholarship 2026
Professional Education Foundation (PEF) provides financial assistance to deserving and financially needy students pursuing higher education.
Limited slots available
Deadline: April 17, 2026
Eligibility Criteria
Regularly enrolled at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
Secured admission on open merit in the current academic program
Demonstrate genuine financial need
Intermediate score must NOT be below 65%
Monthly family income must NOT exceed PKR 75,000/-
Scholarship Benefits
Financial assistance for educational expenses
Need-based support (Zakat) or Donation based on eligibility
Support for Business Management students
Support for Computer Sciences students
Application Process
1
Download and complete the PEF Scholarship Application Form
2
Attach all required supporting documents
3
Submit complete application to Student Financial Aid Office or Treasurer Office
4
Wait for shortlisting and verification
5
Affidavit required after provisional approval
Required Documents
CNIC / B-Form copy
Matriculation Mark Sheet (attested)
Intermediate Mark Sheet (attested)
Income Certificate or Pay Slip of the earning person
Zakat Affidavit (if Zakat is selected)
Email Address and Phone Number
University I.D. Card copy
Two passport size photographs
Last two Electricity Bills copies
Last two Gas Bills copies
Death certificate (if father deceased) / Divorce certificate (if parents divorced)
Domicile copy
Medical Certificate (if any disability)
University Admission Order
Download the application form, complete it, and submit to the Financial Aid Office before the deadline.
Download Application Form
Need-Based Scholarships
Financial assistance for students facing economic hardships to continue their education.
Eligibility Criteria
Family income below PKR 40,000/month
At least 50% marks in last examination
Orphan or special child (priority consideration)
Single parent family (priority)
Scholarship Benefits
Partial to full tuition fee waiver (based on need assessment)
Flexible fee installment plans
Book bank facility
Transportation allowance
Application Process
1
Visit the Student Financial Aid Office
2
Obtain the Financial Aid Application Form
3
Submit with income proof and utility bills
4
Home visit by financial aid committee
5
Need assessment interview
6
Award letter issued within 2 weeks
Visit the Student Financial Aid Office for application forms and assistance.
Visit Student Financial Aid Office
Kinship Scholarships
Special scholarships for children of university employees, armed forces personnel, and government servants.
Eligibility Criteria
Child/spouse of UoBS regular employee (serving/retired/deceased)
Child of Armed Forces personnel (with nomination)
Child of Government Teachers (with NOC)
Minimum 60% marks in last examination
Scholarship Benefits
25% tuition fee waiver for children of UoBS employees
50% tuition fee waiver for children of martyred employees
20% tuition fee waiver for armed forces/teachers' children
Priority in hostel accommodation
Application Process
1
Visit the Student Financial Aid Office
2
Submit Kinship Application Form
3
Attach proof of relationship (B-Form/CNIC)
4
Employee/service certificate from relevant department
5
NOC from parent's employer
6
Verification by Registrar's Office
Required Documents
CNIC/B-Form of applicant
Parent's CNIC
Employee/service certificate
Last academic transcript
Death certificate (if applicable)
Visit the Student Financial Aid Office for application forms and assistance.
Visit Student Financial Aid Office
Application Guidelines
How to apply for scholarships
Application Steps
1
Review eligibility criteria for each scholarship
2
Prepare required documents (transcripts, CNIC, income proof)
3
For PEF Scholarship: Download and complete the application form
4
For Need-Based & Kinship: Visit Student Financial Aid Office
5
Submit before deadline with all attachments
6
Appear for interview if required
7
Check university website for merit lists
Submission Address
Student Financial Aid Office
Treasurer Office
University of Baltistan, Skardu
Required Documents Checklist
Academic transcripts/certificates (attested)
CNIC/B-Form copy
Domicile certificate
Income certificate from union council/employer
Recent passport-size photographs (3 copies)
Character certificate from last institution
Scholarship-specific forms (if applicable)
Utility bills (Electricity & Gas)
Zakat affidavit (if applicable)
Important Notes
• Incomplete or inaccurate applications will not be considered
• False information leads to scholarship cancellation and disciplinary action
• Scholarships cannot be combined unless specified
• Recipients must maintain required academic performance
• Scholarship committee decisions are final
• Only shortlisted candidates may be contacted for further verification
• All fields in the form are mandatory - incomplete forms will not be entertained
• By applying for financial assistance, PEF reserves the right to use the student for fundraising events and/or promotional activities
• Students must share updated contact details; failure to contact may result in scholarship termination
• Zakat affidavit on stamp paper is required after provisional approval
• Only applications received through University will be considered
• Amount approved will be transferred directly to the University on behalf of student's educational expenses
Frequently Asked Questions
Common queries about scholarships
What is the deadline for PEF Scholarship 2026?
The deadline for PEF Scholarship 2026 submission is April 17, 2026. Incomplete or inaccurate applications will not be considered.
What are the eligibility requirements for PEF Scholarship?
To be eligible, you must be regularly enrolled at UoBS on open merit, have intermediate score not below 65%, and monthly family income not exceeding PKR 75,000/-.
Where do I submit the PEF application form?
Completed application forms along with all required documents must be submitted to the Student Financial Aid Office or Treasurer Office at University of Baltistan, Skardu.
How do I apply for Need-Based or Kinship Scholarship?
Please visit the Student Financial Aid Office at the University campus. The office hours are Monday-Friday, 9:00 AM - 4:00 PM.
Do I need to reapply every year?
Most scholarships require annual renewal based on academic performance. Some automatically renew if you maintain the required CGPA, while others require fresh applications.
Are part-time students eligible for scholarships?
Most scholarships are for full-time regular students only. Some need-based scholarships may consider part-time students on a case-by-case basis.
How are PEF scholarship recipients selected?
Selection is based on merit (academic performance with minimum 65% in intermediate), financial need assessment (monthly income below PKR 75,000), and submission of complete and verifiable documents.
What is the difference between Need-Based Support and Donation?
Need-Based Financial Support is Zakat-based, requiring a Zakat affidavit. Donation is non-Zakat support. Both are subject to fund availability. Choose wisely as documents will be reviewed and final decision taken by concerned authority.
Need Help?
Contact our Financial Aid Office for assistance
financialaid@uobs.edu.pk
+92-5815-123456 (Ext. 245)
Monday-Friday, 9:00 AM - 4:00 PM

Functions & Responsibilities of BASR
The Board of Advanced Studies & Research (BASR) is responsible for promoting, coordinating, and supervising postgraduate studies and research activities across the University. It ensures maintenance of academic standards in research programs.
Approve new postgraduate programs (MPhil, PhD) and research initiatives
Monitor quality and standards of research degrees across departments
Approve research proposals, thesis topics, and supervision panels
Recommend policies for research funding, grants and scholarships
Oversee research ethics, integrity and compliance procedures
Coordinate with external research bodies and funding agencies
Review and recommend improvements to research infrastructure
Approve research collaborations, partnerships and MoUs
Supervise admission, progression and examination of research students
Promote research publications, conferences and seminars
Recommend conferment of research degrees to the Senate
Monitor implementation of research policies and guidelines

Department of Mathematics
Exploring Patterns, Solving Problems, Shaping Futures
Established: 2019
HEC Recognized
180+ Students
Introduction
The Department of Mathematics is one of the pioneer disciplines of the university, established in 2019. It comprises highly qualified faculty members, with most actively engaged in research across applied, pure, and computational mathematics. The department has played a leading role in research and teaching within the university, annually producing impressive research articles in international journals. Many faculty members have established research collaborations with prominent scholars at national and international levels. Within a short period, the department initiated its BS program in 2019, followed by the MS program in 2021 and the PhD program in 2023. To date, it has produced more than 10 MS scholars by 2024.
Department Statistics
180+

4

Vision & Mission
Our Vision
Department of Mathematics will be a source for the promotion of problem solving, analytical thinking and utilizing technology. It is our belief that the rapidly changing technological advances have created a fluid and dynamic world for this generation of students so keeping in mind these challenges, vision of mathematics department is to graduate qualified cadres who are equipped with insight and research skills in the field of mathematics and make lasting contribution to the society by helping to solve problems in such diverse fields as medicine, management, economics, government organizations, computer sciences, physics, psychology, engineering and social sciences.
Our Mission
Mathematics responses to the fundamental human endeavour to understand the world. Furthermore, modern technological society relies on developments in these disciplines. Consequently, the Department of Mathematics at Baltistan University, Long Beach, as in most universities, plays a vital and indispensable role in the lives of the students and faculty. The Department’s mission is two-fold: to assist in integrating students into society by educating them in these fields; secondly, to build and foster an excellent faculty, which contributes to the development of mathematics and serves as a resource of mathematical expertise for the people of this state

About University of
Baltistan, Skardu
Empowering minds at the roof of the world since 2010. A premier institution dedicated to academic excellence, research innovation, and community development in Gilgit-Baltistan.
2012
Established
59
Faculty Members
3
Faculties
11
Departments
26
Degree Programs
1800+
Alumni
Our Journey
A Legacy of Excellence at the Roof of the World
University of Baltistan, Skardu was established on August 25, 2017, under the "University of Baltistan Order 2016" as a federal public-sector university. The University facilitates an opportunity for all persons irrespective of gender, religion, race, creed, class, color, or domicile. Earlier, as a higher educational institution, the university started functioning as a sub-campus of Karakoram International University in 2011 with four departments including Computer Science, Modern Languages, Business Management, and Educational Development. The Department of Biological Sciences was initiated in 2017 hence increasing the number of departments to five.
The University of Baltistan, Skardu is a public-sector university and the only higher education institution offering various degree programs in the region. The establishment of this university has a multitude of prospects in the future for the local, national, and international students. Currently, the University offers admissions in BS and Masters Programs in various departments such as Computer Science, Modern Languages, Biological Sciences, Mathematics, Business Management, Tourism and Hospitality, Chemistry, Environmental Science, and Educational Development.
The geostrategic location of the university provides a strong foundation for current and future endeavors. Baltistan is situated in the lap of the two largest mountain ranges, i.e., Himalaya and Karakoram, in the extreme north of Pakistan. The region is composed of several enchanting valleys including Skardu, Khapulu, Shigar, Kharmang, Gultari, and Roundu. Skardu is the capital city of the entire region. The region abounds with natural resources, landscapes, and unique climatic conditions. It hosts some of the largest glacial ranges such as Baltoro, Biafo, Siachen, Trango, and Godwin-Austen. The region is home to five summits which are more than 8000 meters. Among them, K2 is the second-highest peak in the world. In addition, a cluster of 150 world-famous mountain peaks is also located in this terrain. Deosai, the world's highest plateau, is also located in Baltistan. Indus River, one of the longest rivers of the world, creeps crossing different valleys of Baltistan till it rushes towards the North-west into the gorge Roundu and adjacent valleys. Several natural and artificial lakes increase the beauty of this terrain. There are hundreds of thousands of herbal medicinal plants in this region. Other than these, Baltistan is also famous for mining, wildlife, built heritage, unique cultural customs, and games (polo, skiing, etc). The mesmerizing features of Baltistan attract thousands of national and international tourists and researchers every year. Therefore, all the programs and activities in the university are the mirror reflection of enriched local resources of the vicinity. The university plays a pivotal role in the socio-economic and educational development of the region and nation through robust and contextually relevant programs.
HEC Recognized
Category W University

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
External Linkages
Building global partnerships and fostering international collaboration at the University of Baltistan, Skardu
Global Partnerships
Introduction
Under the provision of the University of Baltistan Order 2016, clause 4. Powers and purpose of the University, subsection (viii) and (xvii) the university shall: institute programs for the exchange of students and teachers between the University and other universities, educational institutions and research organizations, inside as well as outside Pakistan” ; and
“cooperate and build linkages with other universities, institutions, public authorities or private organizations, inside as well outside Pakistan, in such manner and for such purposes as it may prescribe”.
With formal functioning of University of Baltistan, in 2018 after arrival of the founding Vice Chancellor, realizing the potential and need of outreach for the new the university, the Directorate of External Linkages was established. The responsibility of Director External Linkages was assigned to Dr. Zakir Hussain Zakir as the founding Director. The directorate reached out expeditiously to create social space of the University among national and international institutions and organizations.
The directorate also strived to register the nascent university on different forums of university ranking. So far 60+ Memorandums of Understanding (MoUs) and letters of agreements have been signed. The primary aim of these MoUs were to introduce the University as a potential collaborating partner for academic linkages, research and development.
Dr. Shafqat Hussain
Director External Linkages
"We live in the global village of digital natives where survival in isolation is not a choice. Located in the highly significant geostrategic location and rich in natural resources and cultural heritage UOBS has enormous potential to attract national and international partners for collaborative joint R&D pursuits. The Directorate is committed and determined to tap these potentials and opportunities with support of our faculty members, management and academia."
director.external.linkages@uobs.edu.pk
Our Framework
Mission, Vision & Objectives
Mission
To create, strengthen, enrich and expand linkages with relevant national and international stakeholders prospective partners, for quality learning opportunities and research practices, through collaborative efforts so that the university becomes a basecamp of research, innovation and a learning site for knowledge creation..
Vision
An institution of higher learning known to global academia and development agencies as a trusted academic partner with whom institutions would feel proud to collaborate.
Objectives
•
Introduce the university as a highly potential academic institution for partnership for R&D in the contextually relevant resources and services
•
Beautify and enrich the real social image of the region and the country as the most beautiful and peaceful places rich in natural and cultural beauties and resources
•
Collaborate with prospective stakeholders for partnership endeavors in learning, research and development
•
Build and maintain a strong link of collaborative efforts with communities and partners for the betterment of the University and the region
•
Translate the partnership commitments into practical outcomes in form of knowledge generation, learning opportunities, research outcomes and innovative products
•
Reach out to international prospective partners for collaboration in areas of mutual interest
•
Formulate partnership deeds for win-win projects without any liability on the university

Admissions
Begin your academic journey at University of Baltistan, Skardu
Introduction
Start your academic journey with us
Welcome to the University of Baltistan, Skardu — a center of academic excellence set against the breathtaking backdrop of the Karakoram. We are committed to transforming potential into achievement by providing an environment where knowledge, innovation, and leadership thrive.
The Admissions Office is your gateway to a future defined by purpose and possibility. Whether you are beginning your undergraduate studies, advancing through postgraduate programs, or pursuing research, our admissions team is here to support you with clarity, transparency, and care.
At the University of Baltistan, we believe education is more than instruction — it is empowerment. Our highly qualified faculty, modern facilities, and outcome-focused learning approach ensure that every student gains the skills, confidence, and vision required to succeed in a rapidly evolving world.
Join a diverse academic community where excellence is expected, integrity is valued, and ambition is nurtured. Your journey toward a meaningful and impactful future begins here.
Our Values
Excellence
Integrity
Diversity
Innovation
Community Engagement

How to Apply
Step-by-step application process
1
Check Eligibility
Review minimum requirements for your desired program
Minimum 45% marks in intermediate/equivalent for undergraduate programs
2
Online Registration
Create account on UOBS admission portal
Visit admissions.uobs.edu.pk and register with valid email
3
Fill Application Form
Complete all sections of the online application
Upload required documents and select program choices
4
Pay Application Fee
Submit application fee through prescribed methods
Fee: Rs. 1000 for undergraduate, Rs. 1500 for postgraduate
5
Submit Documents
Upload scanned copies of required documents
Educational certificates, CNIC, domicile, photographs
6
Track Application
Monitor application status through portal
Regularly check for updates and merit lists
Application Checklist
Valid email address for registration
Scanned copies of required documents
Application fee payment receipt
Program selection finalized

Merit Calculation Process
Merit is calculated based on academic marks and entry test scores
Additional weightage for regional quota as per government policy
Lists are displayed on university notice boards and website
Selected candidates must complete admission formalities within due date
Admission Policy
Rules and regulations
Eligibility Criteria
Minimum 45% marks in previous qualification
Valid CNIC/B-Form
Domicile of Gilgit-Baltistan
No objection certificate for government employees
Selection Process
Academic merit (70% weightage)
Entry test score (30% weightage)
Interview for selected programs
Reserved quotas as per government policy
Documents Required
All educational certificates/degrees
CNIC/B-Form copies
Domicile certificate
Character certificate
8 passport size photographs
General Rules
Admission is subject to verification of documents
False information leads to cancellation
Fee must be paid within due dates
University rules must be followed
International Students
Guidelines for international applicants
Eligibility
Equivalent qualification recognized by IBCC/HEC
Visa Requirements
Student visa with No Objection Certificate from government
English Proficiency
IELTS/TOEFL or equivalent for English medium programs
Health Requirements
Medical fitness certificate and vaccination records
Support Services
✓
International Students Office
✓
Accommodation Assistance
✓
Orientation Programs
✓
Cultural Integration Support
FAQs
Frequently asked questions
General Questions
When do admissions open?
Admissions open twice a year for Fall (July) and Spring (December) semesters.
What is the application fee?
Rs. 1000 for undergraduate and Rs. 1500 for postgraduate programs.
Eligibility
What are the minimum marks required?
Minimum 45% marks in intermediate/equivalent for undergraduate programs.
Documents
What documents are required?
Educational certificates, CNIC, domicile, photographs, and other specified documents.

Department of Business Management, Commerce, and Economics
Empowering Communities Through Innovative Business Education and Excellence
Established: 2013
HEC Recognized
559+ Students
Introduction
Welcome to Department of Business Management, Commerce, and Economics University of Baltistan, Skardu. Today's dynamic business world presents managers with an array of complex and exciting challenges, such as globalization, workforce diversity and scarce resources. The best-selling Introduction to Business Management prepares students to meet these challenges. By the grace of Almighty Allah, the department has established Bachelor and Master Programs for the students who seek to develop their career in business education and research. Our qualified and experienced faculty members have put a transparent and integrated system in position. We want to be the most important business management department in terms of management thinking, theory, practice and useable research. Every student in our BBA, BS (Economics), MS in Management Science programs has a choice to choose one area of interest from, Finance, Marketing, Human Resource Management, and economics. The BBA, BS (Economics) and MS programs in different areas of interest are developed with an objective to produce managers, with strong theoretical knowledge and advanced management skills. The key factors attracting students to Business Management Department are the methodology, quality of teaching and exposure. We strongly follow guidelines by HEC and have made our program possible for working professionals, by offering classes in the evening. The department of Business Management always provides and maintains quality education by using different innovative tools of teaching.

Vision & Mission
Our Vision
Empowering the communities through innovative business education and excellence.
Our Mission
Fostering a professional environment where students are equipped with market-oriented business
education, research acumen, and entrepreneurial skills tailored to the distinctive challenges and
opportunities of mountain economies, thereby shaping leaders who drive sustainable economic growth
locally and globally.
Core Values
Integrity and Ethics
Innovation and Digital Mindset
Entrepreneurial Thinking, Global and Local
Relevance
Leadership and Collaboration and Sustainability &amp; Social Responsibility
