[Policy Document] 
Strategic Development Plan 
 
 (2025–2035) 
 
 
 
 
 
Directorate of Planning and Development 
 
 
University of Baltistan 
Skardu, Pakistan 
August 2025 
 




 
 
Page 2 of 10 
Acronyms 
AI  Artificial Intelligence (technology tool) 
GB  Gilgit-Baltistan (the province like unit of northern Pakistan) 
IQC  Institutional Quality C ircle ( a body of the university constituted to 
oversee for policies and practices in terms of quality standards)   
IT  Information Technology (Internet based resources) 
KPI  Key Performance Indicator (a tool of performance evaluation) 
NGOs  Non-Government Organizations ( mainly serving in academics, 
research and development) 
ODL  Online and Distance Learning ( technology-based learning mode and 
facilities)  
ORIC  Office of Research, Innovation and Commercialization (a Directorate 
of the University)  
PC-1  Project Concept 1 st (the official document of a project proposal for 
government funding) 
PPP  Public Private Partnership (with reference PPP Authority government 
gazette policy) 
QEC  Quality Enhancement Cell (a unit of university management)  
R &D  Research and Development (as role of university) 
SDG  Sustainable Development Goals ( international development targets 
for 2030) 
SIC  Strategic Implementation Committee (a higher power committee of the 
university) 
TICs  Technical Incubation Centers (the centers established by University of 
Baltistan) 
UN  United Nations (refer to all sub organs of UN) 
UoBS  University of Baltistan, Skardu 
 


 
 
Page 3 of 10 
University of Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS) 
Strategic Development Plan (2025–2035) 
Introduction 
University of Baltistan is located at the geostrategic location of Baltistan northern Pakistan 
bordering with China and India. It is at the basecamps of the glaciers of the Third Pole and the 
wonders of the Karakoram and the Himalayas rich in both natural and cultural resources. The 
University is cognizant of the opportunities, resources and challenges and aspiration of the local 
communities. Being the only institution of higher learning, it aspires to play key role in the 
sustainable socio -economic develop ment and wellbeing of the people with a pluralist and 
multidisciplinary approach in research and innovation. Primarily the mandate of the university is 
to produce human resources of required futuristic skills and knowledge. Secondly it has to 
facilitate stakeholders with informed and data driven policies and practices. This policy document 
aims to chalk out the envisioned development plan for the university. 
 
Vision 
"We aspire to foster academic excellence and create an enabling environment to nurture human 
capabilities for sustainable socio-economic development for all." 
Mission Statement 
"To provide inclusive, high-quality education and foster innovative research that leverages local 
resources, technology, and AI to drive sustainable socio -economic development while nurturing 
a pluralistic and progressive culture." 
 
Core Values 
• Human Dignity 
• Moral Sanctity 
• Social Justice 
• Peace and Tolerance 
• Eco-friendliness 
 
Strategic Goals and Objectives 
 
Goal 1: To Ensure Academic Excellence 
• Develop and implement innovative curricula integrating local context with global 
standards. 
• Expand and strengthen Faculties and Disciplines: 


 
 
Page 4 of 10 
1. Natural & Life Sciences – Biotechnology, Climate Sciences & Glaciology,  
Physics, Geosciences and Mineralogy, Nutrition & Agriculture.  
2. Social Sciences & Humanities – Psychology, Special Education, Early 
Childhood Education, Literature and Aesthetics. 
3. Health Sciences (new) – Nursing, Medical Lab Technology, Public Health, 
Pharmacy, allied diplomas/certifications/degrees. 
4. Professional institutions in Medicine (MBBS) and Engineering Degree 
programs and Diplomas (Civil, Mining & Electrical/Electronics, etc.) – 
envisioned programs.  
• Ensure student–faculty ratios as per national and international standards. 
• Organize annual faculty development programs and international exchange initiatives. 
• Provide state of the art academic infrastructure, 
• Operationalize the Public Examination Board. 
• Establishment of multiple institutes and schools of unique programs 
 
Goal 2: To Foster Research Culture and Development 
• Establish Centres of Excellence for R&D focused on: 
o Medicinal plants, biodiversity, and mineral resource utilization. 
o Climate change adaptation, glaciology, and disaster management. 
o AI-driven applied solutions and patents. 
• Strengthen the Office of Research, Innovation & Commercialization (ORIC). 
• Build state-of-the-art Research Laboratories for sustainability and community 
facilitation  
• Forging strategic research partnerships with leading national and international 
universities and research centres, including student exchange programs for academic and 
professional enrichment.
 
Goal 3: Focus on Technology Integration 
• Implement digital transformation initiatives across academic and administrative 
functions.  
• Deploy AI-powered research tools and platforms. 
• Upgrade IT infrastructure and improve high-speed internet connectivity across campus. 


 
 
Page 5 of 10 
• Establish technical incubation centres for skills development, freelancing, and business 
incubation in IT/AI sectors. 
• Develop globally accessible web platforms and mobile applications for ODL services, 
promoting local products, services, and tourism potential. 
• Provide Technology based examination and assessment services by the Examination 
Board, introduce E-assessment practices.  
 
Goal 4: To Ensure Community Engagement 
• Establish outreach programs promoting cultural heritage, environmental conservation, 
and tourism.  
• Develop archives of indigenous knowledge, innovative local solutions and heritage by 
involving community experts, elders and representatives.  
• Offer professional certifications, trainings, and diplomas in contextually relevant 
innovative fields. 
• Build strategic partnerships with local and regional stakeholders, industries, NGOs, 
donor agencies, and UN offices to foster socio-economic uplift.  
 
Goal 5: To Develop Robust Infrastructure  
• Build modern research facilities, smart classrooms, labs, and learning spaces. (PC-1 
for R&D Labs submitted) 
• Establish the Directorate of Works and Estate Care to manage ongoing projects, green 
campus initiative and effective security of campus.  
• Develop renewable energy solutions (solar/hybrid). 
• Construct modern hostels, cafeterias, a faculty guest house, and facilitate Tourism 
Department for Hospitality Services on commercial basis. 
• Establish Green Campus and achieve UI Green Metric rankings. 
• Establish regional centres of Open and Distance Learning (ODL). 
• Enhance capacity of Gilgit sub office as Coordination/Directorate Office to enhance 
coordination with GB’s key stakeholders (Government offices, NGOs, & UN agencies), 
broaden academic affiliations across regional colleges, and fortify future avenues in 
distance learning, project acquisition, and collaborative partnerships. 
 


 
 
Page 6 of 10 
Goal 6: To Ensure Inclusive Governance 
• Ensure openness, transparency and accountability across administrative levels. 
• Ensure shared decision making, 
• Decentralization through empowering concerned offices,  
• Strengthen QEC and IQC for monitoring affiliated colleges and evaluating teaching 
quality. 
• Practice data driven decision making, 
• Obtain annual strategic plans and KPIs from every office head, with performance 
analysed annually. 
 
Goal 7: Ascertain Sustainability (align with SDGs) 
• Improve capacity and retention of skilled human resources,  
• Manage assets, financial resources and practices to attain financial sustainability.  
• Improve the Ecosystem with green ecology, green practices and green energy options.  
•  
 
 


 
 
Page 7 of 10 
Development Plan (Phased Execution) 
Short-term (2025–2027) 
• Launch Faculty of Health Sciences and high-demand departments. 
• Fully operationalize the Examination Board with 70% paperless assessment practices.  
• Establish Coordination Office or Directorate of Afflation to facilitate and enhance 
capacity/quality of affiliated colleges and secondary/ higher secondary schools.  
• Faculty Development Programs. At least two weeks training program will be 
scheduled during winter vacation in national institutions of relevant expertise in areas of 
identified capacity building needs. 
• Establish Center of Excellence for local resource-based research and innovation. 
• Operationalize Directorate of Works and Estate Care to look after the ongoing 
projects, green campus initiatives, repair and maintenance and security matters. 
• Develop 50% of campus premises into green and initiate transition to renewable energy. 
• Build new hostels and faculty guest houses. 
• Facilitate Department of Tourism and Hospitality for Hospitality services under PPP 
initiatives. 
• Upgrade healthcare facilities with doctors, equipment, and medicines. 
• Activate professional training programs targeting youth and working professionals in GB 
by leveraging the diverse expertise of UoBS’s PhD faculty.  
 
Medium-term (2028–2030) 
• Expand departments in Natural & Life Sciences, Social Sciences, and Health 
Sciences. 
• Fully operationalize the sustainability initiatives under TICs, Public Private Partnership 
including Public Flower Park, Gems Value Addition, Tourism services etc. 
• Achieve 80–100% renewable energy coverage. 
• Strengthen national and international research collaborations. 
• Build sports complexes, co-curricular centres, and an innovation hub. 
• Through the Technical Incubation Centres’ (TICs) market local resources, products and 
services globally using modern IT platforms. 
• Develop community outreach programs to promote cultural heritage & environmental 
conservation.  
• Expand the public examination services of the Examination Board throughout GB and 
other corners of the country. 
• Develop state-of-the-art research facilities and laboratories. Will emphasize purpose-
built units with self-sufficient green energy sources (fully solarized/hybrid).  
• The non-construction areas and patches of the campus will be converted into 100% green 
campus with thematic garden, nursery and trees on both sides of the internal roads.  
• Infrastructure development of centres/ schools in other valleys will be initiated. 
 
Long-term (2031–2035) 
• Offer 50% of academic programs through international joint degrees. Establish 
international partnerships for research and academic collaborations. Learning will be 
made boundary less, real-time online classes with international institutions will be 
introduced.  
• Achieve zero-waste recycling and a model green institution. 
• Establish UoBS as a regional leader in glaciology, archaeology, minerals, and tourism 
research. 


 
 
Page 8 of 10 
• Rank among the top 10 universities in Pakistan and top 100 in Asia. 
• Achieve full financial sustainability through diversified revenue streams. 
•  


 
 
Page 9 of 10 
Execution Framework 
1. Oversight & Governance 
• Strategic Implementation Committee (SIC) chaired by VC. 
• Annual Action Plans developed by faculties and directorates. 
• Quarterly reviews and annual performance audits with transparent reporting. 
2. Resource Identification & Allocation 
• Human Resources: Recruit faculty, visiting scholars, and administrative experts per 
HEC standards. 
• Financial Resources: Government funding, international grants, PPPs, alumni 
endowment, and commercial ventures. 
• Technological Resources: AI platforms, renewable energy, advanced IT infrastructure. 
• Physical Resources: Smart classrooms, labs, hostels, guest houses, healthcare units, 
hotel facilities. 
3. Ensuring Availability & Contingency Planning 
• Establish a Resource Mobilization Unit under the SIC. 
• Prioritize high-impact projects during funding constraints. 
• Temporary infrastructure leasing and shared resources through PPP models. 
4. Monitoring & KPIs 
• Annual faculty-student ratio compliance. 
• Research outputs: patents, publications, projects/grants, commercialization successes. 
• Percentage renewable energy adoption and UI Green Metric ranking. 
• Revenue generation through professional trainings and commercial ventures. 
• International collaborations and ranking improvements. 
 
Significance & Impact 
• Addresses GB’s critical needs in healthcare, environment, tourism, and socio-economic 
development. 
• UOBS aspires to serve as a platform, channel and mediator for the sustainable socio-
economic growth of the region in liaison with government, national and international 
stakeholders.  
• Positions UoBS as a Center of Excellence in research, innovation, and sustainability. 
• Generates consistent revenue streams through commercialization and tourism ventures. 
• Empowers students, researchers, and youth with globally competitive skills. 
• Strengthens UoBS’s national and international profile as a hub of innovation and 
excellence. 
 
Conclusion 
The Strategic Framework, Development, and Execution Plan positions the University of 
Baltistan, Skardu (UoBS) to emerge as a premier hub of academic excellence, research, and 
innovation. Through the integration of technology, commitment to inclusivity, and pursuit of 
context-driven research, UoBS is poised to drive regional socio-economic transformation while 
safeguarding the unique cultural and natural heritage of Gilgit-Baltistan. 


 
 
Page 10 of 10 
 
Committee Members:

is pleased to announce the candidates shortlisted for interview for the Volunteer Position of Meningitis Ambassad...
The University of Baltistan, Skardu
is pleased to announce the candidates shortlisted for interview for the Volunteer Position of Meningitis Ambassador
2026 under the project “Youth against
Meningitis: Awareness, Advocacy and Action for World Meningitis Day 2026 in

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
PhD Mathematics
Doctoral program for advanced research in mathematics with focus on pure, applied, and computational mathematics.
Established: 2023
4.0 Year Program
18 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The PhD Mathematics program at the Department of Mathematics, University of Baltistan, Skardu, is the highest level of academic qualification designed to produce independent researchers, scholars, and innovators in the field of mathematics. The program focuses on advanced theoretical knowledge, original research, and significant contributions to pure, applied, and computational mathematics at national and international levels.
Program Highlights
Advanced Research & Innovation
Conduct original and impactful research in specialized areas of pure, applied, or computational mathematics, contributing new knowledge to the field.
Specialized Research Areas
Opportunity to work in advanced domains such as functional analysis, differential equations, mathematical modeling, topology, numerical analysis, and other emerging areas.
Scholarly Development
Develop expertise in academic writing, research methodologies, and critical analysis required for high-level scholarly work.
Publications & Conferences
Encouragement and support to publish research in HEC-recognized and international journals, and present findings at national and international conferences.
Program Information
Program Code
PHDMATH
Duration
4.0 Years
Semesters
8
Credit Hours
18
Established
2023
Department:
Department of Mathematics
Curriculum Structure
Career Paths
Professor / Postdoctoral Fellow / Research Mathematician
Lead advanced research in pure or applied mathematics, publish in top-tier journals, mentor graduate students, and teach at university level. Ideal for those pursuing tenured academic positions.
Lead Data Scientist / AI Research Scientist
Develop novel mathematical models for machine learning, deep learning, and uncertainty quantification. PhD-level expertise in optimization, linear algebra, and fuzzy systems drives innovation in predictive analytics.
Director of Mathematical Content / Learning Scientist
Design PhD-level curricula, adaptive learning systems, and digital assessment tools. Lead R&D in mathematical pedagogy for universities or global EdTech platforms.
Data Scientist
Algorithm Engineer
Cryptographer
Professor
Have Questions About This Program?
Contact our program coordinator for personalized guidance
Dr. Munawar Ali Abbas
Assistant Professor & Head of Department
munawer.abbas@uobs.edu.pk
Apply Now
Contact Admissions

SKARDU
BS Mathematics
The BS Mathematics program provides a comprehensive foundation in mathematical principles, analytical thinking, and problem-solving skills.
Established: 2019
HEC Recognized
4.0 Year Program
130 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The Department of Mathematics at the University of Baltistan, Skardu, is one of the pioneer disciplines of the university, established in 2019.
Program Highlights
Strong Foundation
Comprehensive coverage of calculus, algebra, and mathematical analysis.
Research Opportunities
Engage in cutting-edge research in applied, pure, and computational mathematics.
Analytical Skills
Develop analytical and problem-solving skills applicable in diverse fields.
International Collaboration
Research collaborations with prominent scholars at national and international levels.
Mathematical Modeling
Developing tools to simulate real-world problems and interpret datasets.
Machine Learning and Ai
In-depth exploration supervised/unsupervised learning neural network and their mathmetical optimization
Mathematical Modeling
Developing tools to simulate real-world problems and interpret datasets.
Algorithm and Neural Method
Study the efficiency of algorithm, neural linear algebra and graph theory
Visualization
Data mining and visual representation of complex and high dimensional data
Program Information

BSMATH

4.0 Years

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
MS Mathematics
Advanced program with focus on research and specialization in pure and applied mathematics.
Established: 2021
2.0 Year Program
30 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The MS Mathematics program at the Department of Mathematics, University of Baltistan, Skardu, is designed to provide advanced knowledge and specialized training in pure, applied, and computational mathematics. Building upon undergraduate foundations, the program emphasizes research, innovation, and critical thinking to prepare students for academic, professional, and research-oriented careers.
Program Highlights
Data Science
Mathematical Foundation with computational tools to anaylze complex data sets
Artificial Intelligence
Focus on the core mathematical, statistical and algorithms that driven artificial intelligence
Fuzzy Algebra
Extends classical algebraic structures by replacing crisp set membership with degrees of truth, enabling robust modeling of uncertainty in systems, decision-making, and computation
Program Information
Program Code
MSMATH
Duration
2.0 Years
Semesters
4
Credit Hours
30
Established
2021
Department:
Department of Mathematics
Curriculum Structure
Career Paths

Data Scientist, Machine Learning Analyst, Quantitative Modeler
Academia & Research
PhD Candidate, Postdoctoral Researcher, Professor
Government Statistical Officer
Process official statistics, prepare reports, and support policy-making in departments like planning, agriculture, or public health.
Researcher
Quantitative Analyst
Opertion Research Analyst
Specialized Role

BS Tourism and Hospitality Management
The four-year BS program offers an in-depth and comprehensive education in tourism and hospitality management.
Established: 2022
HEC Recognized
4.0 Year Program
129 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The BS Tourism and Hospitality Management program offers an in-depth and comprehensive education in tourism and hospitality management.
Program Highlights
Industry-Focused Curriculum
Curriculum designed in consultation with industry experts from GB Tourism Department, Hotel Association, and Tour Operator Association.
Practical Training
Hands-on experience through dedicated culinary arts and housekeeping labs.
Strategic Location
Located at the intersection of four great mountain ranges along the historic Silk Route (CPEC).
Industry Partnerships
Collaborations with UNWTO, WTTC, and local tourism organizations for internships and placements.
Industry-Focused Curriculum
Curriculum designed to meet the needs of the tourism and hospitality industry
Practical Training
Hands-on experience through dedicated labs in hospitality operations, housekeeping, front office, and culinary arts
Strategic Location
Located at the intersection of four great mountain ranges along the historic Silk Route (CPEC)
Research Focus
Two research projects in final year to develop research capabilities
Field Experience
Internship opportunity in Semester VII for practical industry exposure
Comprehensive Coverage
Courses covering sustainable tourism, cultural tourism, event management, and destination branding

University of Baltistan
SKARDU
BS Botany
Comprehensive program covering plant biology, ecology, molecular biology, and conservation.
Established: 2018
HEC Recognized
4.0 Year Program
134 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The BS Botany program at the Department of Botany, University of Baltistan, Skardu, was established in 2023 with a vision to harness the high botanical potential of the Baltistan region. The program offers comprehensive study of plant life, from molecular mechanisms to ecosystem dynamics, with special emphasis on the unique flora of the region.
Program Highlights
Comprehensive Curriculum
Covers plant diversity, anatomy, physiology, ecology, molecular biology, and conservation
Regional Focus
Special emphasis on the unique flora and biodiversity of the Baltistan region
Research Opportunities
Hands-on research experience in plant sciences with faculty supervision
Field Work
Extensive field trips to diverse ecological zones in Baltistan
Herbarium
Access to preserved plant specimens for taxonomy and biodiversity studies
Modern Labs
State-of-the-art laboratories for molecular biology, phytochemistry, and plant pathology
Specializations
Plant Physiology
Plant Ecology
Molecular Botany
Mycology and Plant Pathology
Phytochemistry
Program Information
Program Code
BSBOT

HEC Recognized
4.0 Year Program
130 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The BS Economics program offers comprehensive education in economic theory, quantitative methods, and applied economics.

Quantitative Methods

Applied Economics

Policy Analysis

Research Focus

Business Decision-Making

BS Environmental Science
Comprehensive program covering environmental science fundamentals, ecology, climate change, and sustainable development.
Established: 2021
HEC Recognized
4.0 Year Program
136 Credit Hours
Program Overview
The BS Environmental Science program offers comprehensive study of environmental systems, ecology, climate change, and sustainable development.
Program Highlights
Ecohydrology

Climate Change
Regional impacts and adaptation strategies.
